# Fix: GitHub Copilot provider — Responses API support for gpt-5.4+

## Context

GitHub Copilot provider (`crates/providers/src/github_copilot.rs`) hardcodes `/chat/completions` for all models. Newer OpenAI models (gpt-5.4, gpt-5.4-pro, gpt-5.2-pro) only support the Responses API (`/responses`), returning HTTP 400 with `unsupported_api_for_model`. See issue #392.

The OpenAI provider already has Responses API support via WebSocket, but that's gated behind `api.openai.com` domain checks. The Copilot provider needs its own Responses API path using HTTP+SSE (simpler, no WebSocket pool needed).

## Approach

Model-based routing with error-based fallback: dispatch to `/responses` for known models, and detect `unsupported_api_for_model` errors for unknown future models.

## Files to modify

### 1. `crates/providers/src/openai_compat.rs` — shared utilities

- **Move `split_responses_instructions_and_input()`** from `OpenAiProvider` impl to a free function here. It has no `self` dependency — just splits system messages into `instructions` and passes the rest through `to_responses_input()`.

- **Add `responses_output_index()`** — extracted from `ws_output_index()` in `openai.rs` (resolves `output_index` / `item_index` / `index` from a Responses API event). Both providers can share it.

- **Add `process_responses_sse_line()`** — new function that processes a single SSE `data:` line from a Responses API stream. Returns `SseLineResult` (reusing existing enum). Handles event types:
  - `response.output_text.delta` → `StreamEvent::Delta`
  - `response.output_item.added` (type=function_call) → `StreamEvent::ToolCallStart`
  - `response.function_call_arguments.delta` → `StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta`
  - `response.function_call_arguments.done` → `StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete`
  - `response.completed` → parse usage, return `SseLineResult::Done`
  - `error` / `response.failed` → `StreamEvent::Error`

- **Add `ResponsesStreamState`** — state struct for Responses API SSE streaming (tool_calls map, completed_tool_calls set, current_tool_index, usage tokens). Similar to the local variables in `stream_with_tools_websocket()`.

- **Add `finalize_responses_stream()`** — like `finalize_stream()` but for Responses API state (emits pending ToolCallComplete + Done with usage).

- **Add `parse_responses_completion()`** — parse a non-streaming Responses API JSON response into `CompletionResponse`. Handles `output` array with `message` and `function_call` items.

### 2. `crates/providers/src/openai.rs` — update to use shared utilities

- Change `Self::split_responses_instructions_and_input(messages)` → `split_responses_instructions_and_input(messages)` (imported from `openai_compat`)
- Change `ws_output_index()` → `responses_output_index()` (imported from `openai_compat`)
- Remove the now-moved functions from the `OpenAiProvider` impl

### 3. `crates/providers/src/github_copilot.rs` — add Responses API

**Add `needs_responses_api(model: &str) -> bool`:**
```rust
fn needs_responses_api(model: &str) -> bool {
    let m = model.to_ascii_lowercase();
    m.starts_with("gpt-5.4")
        || m == "gpt-5.2-pro"
        || m.starts_with("codex-")
}
```

**Add `is_responses_api_required_error(body: &str) -> bool`:**
Check for `"unsupported_api_for_model"` or `"not accessible via the /chat/completions"` in the error response.

**Add `complete_responses()`:**
- POST to `{COPILOT_API_BASE}/responses` (non-streaming)
- Build request body using `split_responses_instructions_and_input()`, `to_responses_api_tools()`
- Parse response using `parse_responses_completion()`

**Modify `complete()`:**
- If `needs_responses_api(&self.model)`, dispatch directly to `complete_responses()`
- Otherwise, try chat completions. On 400 error with `is_responses_api_required_error()`, retry with `complete_responses()`

**Add `stream_responses_api()`:**
- POST to `{COPILOT_API_BASE}/responses` with `"stream": true`
- Build request body same as `complete_responses()` but with stream options
- Parse SSE events using `process_responses_sse_line()` + `ResponsesStreamState`
- Follow existing SSE parsing pattern (byte_stream → buf → line splitting → event processing)

**Modify `stream_with_tools()`:**
- If `needs_responses_api(&self.model)`, dispatch to `stream_responses_api()`
- Otherwise, use existing chat completions streaming
- Add error detection: if 400 with `is_responses_api_required_error()`, redirect to `stream_responses_api()` (detected before streaming starts, in the error_for_status_ref check)

**Update `COPILOT_MODELS`:**
- Add `("gpt-5.4", "GPT-5.4 (Copilot)")`, `("gpt-5.4-pro", "GPT-5.4 Pro (Copilot)")`, `("gpt-5.2-pro", "GPT-5.2 Pro (Copilot)")`

**Update `MockCopilotProvider` in tests:**
- Add mock `/responses` endpoint
- Add test for `needs_responses_api()` function
- Add test for `is_responses_api_required_error()`
- Add test for `complete_responses()` with mock
- Add test for error-based fallback from chat completions to responses API
- Add streaming test for responses API

### 4. `crates/providers/src/openai_compat.rs` — tests

- Add tests for `split_responses_instructions_and_input()`
- Add tests for `process_responses_sse_line()` covering all event types

     1→//! GitHub Copilot provider.
     2→//!
     3→//! Authentication uses the GitHub device-flow OAuth to obtain a GitHub token,
     4→//! then exchanges it for a short-lived Copilot API token via
     5→//! `https://api.github.com/copilot_internal/v2/token`.
     6→//!
     7→//! The Copilot API itself is OpenAI-compatible (`/chat/completions`).
     8→

    10→
    11→use {
    12→    async_trait::async_trait,
    13→    futures::StreamExt,
    14→    moltis_oauth::{OAuthTokens, TokenStore},
    15→    secrecy::{ExposeSecret, Secret},
    16→    tokio_stream::Stream,
    17→    tracing::{debug, trace, warn},

    23→        parse_tool_calls, process_openai_sse_line, to_openai_tools,
    24→    },
    25→    moltis_agents::model::{ChatMessage, CompletionResponse, LlmProvider, StreamEvent},
    26→};

    35→const COPILOT_TOKEN_URL: &str = "https://api.github.com/copilot_internal/v2/token";

    46→// ── Device flow types ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    48→#[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]
    49→pub struct DeviceCodeResponse {

    54→}
    55→

    61→

    68→// ── Provider ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    69→
    70→pub struct GitHubCopilotProvider {
    71→    model: String,
    72→    client: &'static reqwest::Client,
    73→    token_store: TokenStore,
    74→}
    75→
    76→impl GitHubCopilotProvider {
    77→    pub fn new(model: String) -> Self {
    78→        Self {
    79→            model,
    80→            client: crate::shared_http_client(),
    81→            token_store: TokenStore::new(),
    82→        }
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    /// Start the GitHub device-flow: request a device code from GitHub.
    86→    pub async fn request_device_code(
    87→        client: &reqwest::Client,
    88→    ) -> anyhow::Result<DeviceCodeResponse> {
    89→        let resp = client
    90→            .post(GITHUB_DEVICE_CODE_URL)
    91→            .header("Accept", "application/json")
    92→            .form(&[("client_id", GITHUB_CLIENT_ID), ("scope", "")])
    93→            .send()
    94→            .await?;
    95→
    96→        if !resp.status().is_success() {
    97→            let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
    98→            anyhow::bail!("GitHub device code request failed: {body}");
    99→        }
   100→
   101→        Ok(resp.json().await?)
   102→    }
   103→
   104→    /// Poll GitHub for the access token after the user has entered the code.
   105→    pub async fn poll_for_token(
   106→        client: &reqwest::Client,
   107→        device_code: &str,
   108→        interval: u64,
   109→    ) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
   110→        loop {
   111→            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(interval)).await;
   112→
   113→            let resp = client
   114→                .post(GITHUB_TOKEN_URL)
   115→                .header("Accept", "application/json")
   116→                .form(&[
   117→                    ("client_id", GITHUB_CLIENT_ID),
   118→                    ("device_code", device_code),
   119→                    ("grant_type", "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:device_code"),
   120→                ])
   121→                .send()
   122→                .await?;
   123→
   124→            let body: GithubTokenResponse = resp.json().await?;
   125→
   126→            if let Some(token) = body.access_token {
   127→                return Ok(token);
   128→            }
   129→
   130→            match body.error.as_deref() {
   131→                Some("authorization_pending") => continue,
   132→                Some("slow_down") => {
   133→                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
   134→                    continue;
   135→                },
   136→                Some(err) => anyhow::bail!("GitHub device flow error: {err}"),
   137→                None => anyhow::bail!("unexpected response from GitHub token endpoint"),
   138→            }
   139→        }
   140→    }
   141→
   142→    /// Get a valid Copilot API token, exchanging the GitHub token if needed.
   143→    async fn get_valid_copilot_token(&self) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
   144→        fetch_valid_copilot_token_with_fallback(self.client, &self.token_store).await
   145→    }
   146→}
   147→
   148→fn home_token_store_if_different() -> Option<TokenStore> {
   149→    let home = moltis_config::user_global_config_dir_if_different()?;
   150→    Some(TokenStore::with_path(home.join("oauth_tokens.json")))
   151→}
   152→
   153→fn token_store_with_provider_tokens(primary: &TokenStore) -> Option<TokenStore> {
   154→    debug!("checking primary token store for {PROVIDER_NAME}");
   155→    if primary.load(PROVIDER_NAME).is_some() {
   156→        debug!("found {PROVIDER_NAME} tokens in primary store");
   157→        return Some(primary.clone());
   158→    }
   159→    if let Some(home_store) = home_token_store_if_different() {
   160→        debug!("checking home token store for {PROVIDER_NAME}");
   161→        if home_store.load(PROVIDER_NAME).is_some() {
   162→            debug!("found {PROVIDER_NAME} tokens in home store");
   163→            return Some(home_store);
   164→        }
   165→    }
   166→    debug!("{PROVIDER_NAME} tokens not found in any store");
   167→    None
   168→}
   169→
   170→/// Check if we have stored GitHub tokens for Copilot.
   171→pub fn has_stored_tokens() -> bool {
   172→    let found = token_store_with_provider_tokens(&TokenStore::new()).is_some();
   173→    if found {
   174→        debug!("{PROVIDER_NAME} stored tokens found");
   175→    } else {
   176→        debug!("{PROVIDER_NAME} stored tokens not found");
   177→    }
   178→    found
   179→}
   180→
   181→/// Known Copilot models.
   182→/// The list is intentionally broad; if a model isn't available for the user's
   183→/// plan Copilot will return an error.
   184→pub const COPILOT_MODELS: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
   185→    ("gpt-4o", "GPT-4o (Copilot)"),
   186→    ("gpt-4.1", "GPT-4.1 (Copilot)"),
   187→    ("gpt-4.1-mini", "GPT-4.1 Mini (Copilot)"),
   188→    ("gpt-4.1-nano", "GPT-4.1 Nano (Copilot)"),
   189→    ("o1", "o1 (Copilot)"),
   190→    ("o1-mini", "o1-mini (Copilot)"),
   191→    ("o3-mini", "o3-mini (Copilot)"),
   192→    ("claude-sonnet-4", "Claude Sonnet 4 (Copilot)"),
   193→    ("gemini-2.0-flash", "Gemini 2.0 Flash (Copilot)"),
   194→];
   195→
   196→async fn fetch_valid_copilot_token(
   197→    client: &reqwest::Client,
   198→    token_store: &TokenStore,
   199→) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
   200→    let tokens = token_store.load(PROVIDER_NAME).ok_or_else(|| {
   201→        anyhow::anyhow!("not logged in to github-copilot — run OAuth device flow first")
   202→    })?;
   203→
   204→    // The `access_token` stored is the GitHub user token.
   205→    // We exchange it for a short-lived Copilot API token and cache it.
   206→    if let Some(copilot_tokens) = token_store.load("github-copilot-api")
   207→        && let Some(expires_at) = copilot_tokens.expires_at
   208→    {
   209→        let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   210→            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   211→            .unwrap_or_default()
   212→            .as_secs();
   213→        if now + 60 < expires_at {
   214→            return Ok(copilot_tokens.access_token.expose_secret().clone());
   215→        }
   216→    }
   217→
   218→    let resp = client
   219→        .get(COPILOT_TOKEN_URL)
   220→        .header(
   221→            "Authorization",
   222→            format!("token {}", tokens.access_token.expose_secret()),
   223→        )
   224→        .header("Accept", "application/json")
   225→        .header(
   226→            "User-Agent",
   227→            "moltis/0.1.0 (GitHub Copilot compatible client)",
   228→        )
   229→        .send()
   230→        .await?;
   231→
   232→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
   233→        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   234→        anyhow::bail!("Copilot token exchange failed: {body}");
   235→    }
   236→
   237→    let copilot_resp: CopilotTokenResponse = resp.json().await?;
   238→    let _ = token_store.save("github-copilot-api", &OAuthTokens {
   239→        access_token: Secret::new(copilot_resp.token.clone()),
   240→        refresh_token: None,
   241→        id_token: None,
   242→        account_id: None,
   243→        expires_at: Some(copilot_resp.expires_at),
   244→    });
   245→
   246→    Ok(copilot_resp.token)
   247→}
   248→
   249→async fn fetch_valid_copilot_token_with_fallback(
   250→    client: &reqwest::Client,
   251→    primary_store: &TokenStore,
   252→) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
   253→    let Some(token_store) = token_store_with_provider_tokens(primary_store) else {
   254→        anyhow::bail!("not logged in to github-copilot — run OAuth device flow first");
   255→    };
   256→    fetch_valid_copilot_token(client, &token_store).await
   257→}
   258→
   259→pub fn default_model_catalog() -> Vec<super::DiscoveredModel> {
   260→    COPILOT_MODELS
   261→        .iter()
   262→        .map(|(id, name)| super::DiscoveredModel::new(*id, *name))
   263→        .collect()
   264→}
   265→
   266→fn normalize_display_name(model_id: &str, display_name: Option<&str>) -> String {
   267→    let normalized = display_name
   268→        .map(str::trim)
   269→        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
   270→        .unwrap_or(model_id);
   271→    if normalized == model_id {
   272→        model_id.to_string()
   273→    } else {
   274→        normalized.to_string()
   275→    }
   276→}
   277→
   278→fn is_likely_model_id(model_id: &str) -> bool {
   279→    if model_id.is_empty() || model_id.len() > 120 {
   280→        return false;
   281→    }
   282→    if model_id.chars().any(char::is_whitespace) {
   283→        return false;
   284→    }
   285→    model_id
   286→        .chars()
   287→        .all(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(ch, '-' | '_' | '.' | ':'))
   288→}
   289→
   290→fn parse_model_entry(entry: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<super::DiscoveredModel> {
   291→    let obj = entry.as_object()?;
   292→    let model_id = obj
   293→        .get("id")
   294→        .or_else(|| obj.get("slug"))
   295→        .or_else(|| obj.get("model"))
   296→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)?;
   297→
   298→    if !is_likely_model_id(model_id) {
   299→        return None;
   300→    }
   301→
   302→    let display_name = obj
   303→        .get("display_name")
   304→        .or_else(|| obj.get("displayName"))
   305→        .or_else(|| obj.get("name"))
   306→        .or_else(|| obj.get("title"))
   307→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
   308→
   309→    let created_at = obj.get("created").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_i64);
   310→
   311→    Some(
   312→        super::DiscoveredModel::new(model_id, normalize_display_name(model_id, display_name))
   313→            .with_created_at(created_at),
   314→    )
   315→}
   316→
   317→fn collect_candidate_arrays<'a>(
   318→    value: &'a serde_json::Value,
   319→    out: &mut Vec<&'a serde_json::Value>,
   320→) {
   321→    match value {
   322→        serde_json::Value::Array(items) => out.extend(items),
   323→        serde_json::Value::Object(map) => {
   324→            for key in ["models", "data", "items", "results", "available"] {
   325→                if let Some(nested) = map.get(key) {
   326→                    collect_candidate_arrays(nested, out);
   327→                }
   328→            }
   329→        },
   330→        _ => {},
   331→    }
   332→}
   333→
   334→fn parse_models_payload(value: &serde_json::Value) -> Vec<super::DiscoveredModel> {
   335→    let mut candidates = Vec::new();
   336→    collect_candidate_arrays(value, &mut candidates);
   337→
   338→    let mut models = Vec::new();
   339→    let mut seen = HashSet::new();
   340→    for entry in candidates {
   341→        if let Some(model) = parse_model_entry(entry)
   342→            && seen.insert(model.id.clone())
   343→        {
   344→            models.push(model);
   345→        }
   346→    }
   347→
   348→    // Sort by created_at descending (newest first). Models without a
   349→    // timestamp are placed after those with one, preserving relative order.
   350→    models.sort_by(|a, b| match (a.created_at, b.created_at) {
   351→        (Some(a_ts), Some(b_ts)) => b_ts.cmp(&a_ts), // newest first
   352→        (Some(_), None) => std::cmp::Ordering::Less, // timestamp before no-timestamp
   353→        (None, Some(_)) => std::cmp::Ordering::Greater,
   354→        (None, None) => std::cmp::Ordering::Equal,
   355→    });
   356→
   357→    models
   358→}
   359→
   360→async fn fetch_models_from_api(
   361→    client: &reqwest::Client,
   362→    access_token: String,
   363→) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   364→    let response = client
   365→        .get(COPILOT_MODELS_ENDPOINT)
   366→        .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {access_token}"))
   367→        .header("Accept", "application/json")
   368→        .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   369→        .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   370→        .send()
   371→        .await?;
   372→    let status = response.status();
   373→    let body = response.text().await?;
   374→    if !status.is_success() {
   375→        anyhow::bail!("copilot models API error HTTP {status}");
   376→    }
   377→    let payload: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)?;
   378→    let models = parse_models_payload(&payload);
   379→    if models.is_empty() {
   380→        anyhow::bail!("copilot models API returned no models");
   381→    }
   382→    Ok(models)
   383→}
   384→
   385→/// Spawn model discovery in a background thread and return the receiver
   386→/// immediately, without blocking.
   387→pub fn start_model_discovery() -> mpsc::Receiver<anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>>> {
   388→    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   389→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   390→        let result = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
   391→            .enable_all()
   392→            .build()
   393→            .map_err(anyhow::Error::from)
   394→            .and_then(|rt| {
   395→                rt.block_on(async {
   396→                    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
   397→                        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))
   398→                        .build()?;
   399→                    let token_store = TokenStore::new();
   400→                    let access_token =
   401→                        fetch_valid_copilot_token_with_fallback(&client, &token_store).await?;
   402→                    fetch_models_from_api(&client, access_token).await
   403→                })
   404→            });
   405→        let _ = tx.send(result);
   406→    });
   407→    rx
   408→}
   409→
   410→fn fetch_models_blocking() -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   411→    start_model_discovery()
   412→        .recv()
   413→        .map_err(|err| anyhow::anyhow!("copilot model discovery worker failed: {err}"))?
   414→}
   415→
   416→pub fn live_models() -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   417→    let models = fetch_models_blocking()?;
   418→    debug!(
   419→        model_count = models.len(),
   420→        "loaded github-copilot live models"
   421→    );
   422→    Ok(models)
   423→}
   424→
   425→pub fn available_models() -> Vec<super::DiscoveredModel> {
   426→    let fallback = default_model_catalog();
   427→    let discovered = match live_models() {
   428→        Ok(models) => models,
   429→        Err(err) => {
   430→            let msg = err.to_string();
   431→            if msg.contains("not logged in") || msg.contains("tokens not found") {
   432→                debug!(error = %err, "github-copilot not configured, using fallback catalog");
   433→            } else {
   434→                warn!(error = %err, "failed to fetch github-copilot models, using fallback catalog");
   435→            }
   436→            return fallback;
   437→        },
   438→    };
   439→
   440→    super::merge_discovered_with_fallback_catalog(discovered, fallback)
   441→}
   442→
   443→// ── LlmProvider impl ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   444→
   445→#[async_trait]
   446→impl LlmProvider for GitHubCopilotProvider {
   447→    fn name(&self) -> &str {
   448→        PROVIDER_NAME
   449→    }
   450→
   451→    fn id(&self) -> &str {
   452→        &self.model
   453→    }
   454→
   455→    fn supports_tools(&self) -> bool {
   456→        super::supports_tools_for_model(&self.model)
   457→    }
   458→
   459→    async fn complete(
   460→        &self,
   461→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   462→        tools: &[serde_json::Value],
   463→    ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   464→        let token = self.get_valid_copilot_token().await?;
   465→
   466→        let openai_messages: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   467→            messages.iter().map(ChatMessage::to_openai_value).collect();
   468→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   469→            "model": self.model,
   470→            "messages": openai_messages,
   471→        });
   472→
   473→        if !tools.is_empty() {
   474→            body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(tools));
   475→        }
   476→
   477→        debug!(
   478→            model = %self.model,
   479→            messages_count = messages.len(),
   480→            tools_count = tools.len(),
   481→            "github-copilot complete request"
   482→        );
   483→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "github-copilot request body");
   484→
   485→        let http_resp = self
   486→            .client
   487→            .post(format!("{COPILOT_API_BASE}/chat/completions"))
   488→            .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {token}"))
   489→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   490→            .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   491→            .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   492→            .json(&body)
   493→            .send()
   494→            .await?;
   495→
   496→        let status = http_resp.status();
   497→        if !status.is_success() {
   498→            let retry_after_ms = super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   499→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   500→            warn!(status = %status, body = %body_text, "github-copilot API error");
   501→            anyhow::bail!(
   502→                "{}",
   503→                super::with_retry_after_marker(
   504→                    format!("GitHub Copilot API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   505→                    retry_after_ms,
   506→                )
   507→            );
   508→        }
   509→
   510→        let resp = http_resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;
   511→        trace!(response = %resp, "github-copilot raw response");
   512→
   513→        let message = &resp["choices"][0]["message"];
   514→
   515→        let text = message["content"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string());
   516→        let tool_calls = parse_tool_calls(message);
   517→
   518→        let usage = parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(&resp).unwrap_or_default();
   519→
   520→        Ok(CompletionResponse {
   521→            text,
   522→            tool_calls,
   523→            usage,
   524→        })
   525→    }
   526→
   527→    #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   528→    fn stream(
   529→        &self,
   530→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   531→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   532→        self.stream_with_tools(messages, vec![])
   533→    }
   534→
   535→    #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   536→    fn stream_with_tools(
   537→        &self,
   538→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   539→        tools: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
   540→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   541→        Box::pin(async_stream::stream! {
   542→            let token = match self.get_valid_copilot_token().await {
   543→                Ok(t) => t,
   544→                Err(e) => {
   545→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   546→                    return;
   547→                }
   548→            };
   549→
   550→            let openai_messages: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   551→                messages.iter().map(ChatMessage::to_openai_value).collect();
   552→            let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   553→                "model": self.model,
   554→                "messages": openai_messages,
   555→                "stream": true,
   556→                "stream_options": { "include_usage": true },
   557→            });
   558→
   559→            if !tools.is_empty() {
   560→                body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(&tools));
   561→            }
   562→
   563→            debug!(
   564→                model = %self.model,
   565→                messages_count = openai_messages.len(),
   566→                tools_count = tools.len(),
   567→                "github-copilot stream_with_tools request"
   568→            );
   569→            trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "github-copilot stream request body");
   570→
   571→            let resp = match self
   572→                .client
   573→                .post(format!("{COPILOT_API_BASE}/chat/completions"))
   574→                .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {token}"))
   575→                .header("content-type", "application/json")
   576→                .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   577→                .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   578→                .json(&body)
   579→                .send()
   580→                .await
   581→            {
   582→                Ok(r) => {
   583→                    if let Err(e) = r.error_for_status_ref() {
   584→                        let status = e.status().map(|s| s.as_u16()).unwrap_or(0);
   585→                        let retry_after_ms = super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(r.headers());
   586→                        let body_text = r.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   587→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(super::with_retry_after_marker(
   588→                            format!("HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   589→                            retry_after_ms,
   590→                        ));
   591→                        return;
   592→                    }
   593→                    r
   594→                }
   595→                Err(e) => {
   596→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   597→                    return;
   598→                }
   599→            };
   600→
   601→            let mut byte_stream = resp.bytes_stream();
   602→            let mut buf = String::new();
   603→            let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
   604→
   605→            while let Some(chunk) = byte_stream.next().await {
   606→                let chunk = match chunk {
   607→                    Ok(c) => c,
   608→                    Err(e) => {
   609→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   610→                        return;
   611→                    }
   612→                };
   613→                buf.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&chunk));
   614→
   615→                while let Some(pos) = buf.find('\n') {
   616→                    let line = buf[..pos].trim().to_string();
   617→                    buf = buf[pos + 1..].to_string();
   618→
   619→                    if line.is_empty() {
   620→                        continue;
   621→                    }
   622→
   623→                    let Some(data) = line
   624→                        .strip_prefix("data: ")
   625→                        .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   626→                    else {
   627→                        continue;
   628→                    };
   629→
   630→                    match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   631→                        SseLineResult::Done => {
   632→                            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   633→                                yield event;
   634→                            }
   635→                            return;
   636→                        }
   637→                        SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   638→                            for event in events {
   639→                                yield event;
   640→                            }
   641→                        }
   642→                        SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   643→                    }
   644→                }
   645→            }
   646→
   647→            let line = buf.trim().to_string();
   648→            if !line.is_empty()
   649→                && let Some(data) = line
   650→                    .strip_prefix("data: ")
   651→                    .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   652→            {
   653→                match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   654→                    SseLineResult::Done => {
   655→                        for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   656→                            yield event;
   657→                        }
   658→                        return;
   659→                    }
   660→                    SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   661→                        for event in events {
   662→                            yield event;
   663→                        }
   664→                    }
   665→                    SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   666→                }
   667→            }
   668→
   669→            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   670→                yield event;
   671→            }
   672→        })
   673→    }
   674→}

   676→#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]
   677→#[cfg(test)]
   678→mod tests {
   679→    use super::*;
   680→
   681→    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
   682→
   683→    use axum::{Router, extract::Request, routing::post};
   684→
   685→    /// Captured request data for assertions.
   686→    #[derive(Default, Clone)]
   687→    struct CapturedRequest {
   688→        headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
   689→        body: Option<serde_json::Value>,
   690→    }
   691→
   692→    /// Start a mock HTTP server, returning (base_url, captured_requests).
   693→    async fn start_mock_with_capture(
   694→        response_body: serde_json::Value,
   695→    ) -> (String, Arc<Mutex<Vec<CapturedRequest>>>) {
   696→        let captured: Arc<Mutex<Vec<CapturedRequest>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
   697→        let captured_clone = captured.clone();
   698→        let resp_body = response_body.clone();
   699→
   700→        let app = Router::new().route(
   701→            "/chat/completions",
   702→            post(move |req: Request| {
   703→                let cap = captured_clone.clone();
   704→                let resp = resp_body.clone();
   705→                async move {
   706→                    let headers: Vec<(String, String)> = req
   707→                        .headers()
   708→                        .iter()
   709→                        .map(|(k, v)| {
   710→                            (k.as_str().to_string(), v.to_str().unwrap_or("").to_string())
   711→                        })
   712→                        .collect();
   713→
   714→                    let body_bytes = axum::body::to_bytes(req.into_body(), 1024 * 1024)
   715→                        .await
   716→                        .unwrap_or_default();
   717→                    let body: Option<serde_json::Value> = serde_json::from_slice(&body_bytes).ok();
   718→
   719→                    cap.lock().unwrap().push(CapturedRequest { headers, body });
   720→
   721→                    axum::Json(resp)
   722→                }
   723→            }),
   724→        );
   725→
   726→        let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
   727→        let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
   728→        tokio::spawn(async move {
   729→            axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
   730→        });
   731→
   732→        (format!("http://{addr}"), captured)
   733→    }
   734→
   735→    fn mock_completion_response() -> serde_json::Value {
   736→        serde_json::json!({
   737→            "choices": [{
   738→                "message": {
   739→                    "content": "Hello from Copilot!",
   740→                    "role": "assistant"
   741→                }
   742→            }],
   743→            "usage": {
   744→                "prompt_tokens": 10,
   745→                "completion_tokens": 5
   746→            }
   747→        })
   748→    }
   749→

   751→    /// Copilot API, and doesn't need stored OAuth tokens.

   753→        MockCopilotProvider {
   754→            model: model.to_string(),
   755→            client: reqwest::Client::new(),
   756→            base_url: base_url.to_string(),
   757→        }
   758→    }
   759→

   761→    /// token instead of the token store.

   763→        model: String,
   764→        client: reqwest::Client,
   765→        base_url: String,

   767→
   768→    impl MockCopilotProvider {

   771→            messages: &[serde_json::Value],

   773→        ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   774→            let token=[REDACTED]";
   775→
   776→            let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   777→                "model": self.model,

   779→            });
   780→
   781→            if !tools.is_empty() {
   782→                body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(tools));
   783→            }
   784→
   785→            let http_resp = self
   786→                .client
   787→                .post(format!("{}/chat/completions", self.base_url))
   788→                .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {token}"))
   789→                .header("content-type", "application/json")
   790→                .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   791→                .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   792→                .json(&body)
   793→                .send()
   794→                .await?;
   795→
   796→            let status = http_resp.status();
   797→            if !status.is_success() {
   798→                let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   799→                anyhow::bail!("Copilot API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}");
   800→            }
   801→
   802→            let resp = http_resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;
   803→            let message = &resp["choices"][0]["message"];
   804→            let text = message["content"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string());
   805→            let tool_calls = parse_tool_calls(message);
   806→            let usage = parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(&resp).unwrap_or_default();
   807→
   808→            Ok(CompletionResponse {
   809→                text,
   810→                tool_calls,
   811→                usage,
   812→            })
   813→        }
   814→    }

   856→    #[tokio::test]
   857→    async fn complete_sends_required_headers() {
   858→        let (base_url, captured) = start_mock_with_capture(mock_completion_response()).await;
   859→        let provider = mock_provider(&base_url, "gpt-4o");
   860→
   861→        let messages = vec![serde_json::json!({"role": "user", "content": "hi"})];
   862→        let result = provider.complete(&messages, &[]).await;
   863→        assert!(result.is_ok());
   864→
   865→        let reqs = captured.lock().unwrap();
   866→        assert_eq!(reqs.len(), 1);
   867→
   868→        let req = &reqs[0];
   869→
   870→        // Verify required headers
   871→        let has_editor_version = req
   872→            .headers
   873→            .iter()
   874→            .any(|(k, v)| k == "editor-version" && v == EDITOR_VERSION);
   875→        assert!(
   876→            has_editor_version,
   877→            "missing Editor-Version header; got: {:?}",
   878→            req.headers
   879→        );
   880→
   881→        let has_user_agent = req
   882→            .headers
   883→            .iter()
   884→            .any(|(k, v)| k == "user-agent" && v == COPILOT_USER_AGENT);
   885→        assert!(
   886→            has_user_agent,
   887→            "missing User-Agent header; got: {:?}",
   888→            req.headers
   889→        );
   890→
   891→        let has_auth = req
   892→            .headers
   893→            .iter()
   894→            .any(|(k, v)| k == "authorization" && v == "Bearer mock-copilot-token");
   895→        assert!(has_auth, "missing Authorization header");
   896→
   897→        let has_content_type = req
   898→            .headers
   899→            .iter()
   900→            .any(|(k, v)| k == "content-type" && v == "application/json");
   901→        assert!(has_content_type, "missing content-type header");
   902→    }

     1→//! Shared helpers for OpenAI-compatible streaming with tools.
     2→//!
     3→//! This module provides reusable functions for parsing OpenAI-style SSE streams
     4→//! that include tool calls. Used by openai.rs, github_copilot.rs, and kimi_code.rs.
     5→
     6→use std::collections::HashMap;
     7→
     8→use {serde::Serialize, tracing::trace};
     9→
    10→use moltis_agents::model::{ChatMessage, StreamEvent, ToolCall, Usage, UserContent};
    11→
    12→// ============================================================================
    13→// OpenAI Tool Schema Types
    14→// ============================================================================
    15→// These types enforce the correct structure for OpenAI-compatible APIs.
    16→// Using typed structs instead of manual JSON prevents missing fields at compile time.
    17→//
    18→// References:
    19→// - Chat Completions: https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling
    20→// - Responses API: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-foundry/openai/how-to/responses
    21→// ============================================================================
    22→
    23→/// Chat Completions API tool format (nested under "function").
    24→///
    25→/// ```json
    26→/// { "type": "function", "function": { "name": "...", ... } }
    27→/// ```
    28→#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
    29→pub struct ChatCompletionsTool {
    30→    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    31→    pub tool_type: &'static str,
    32→    pub function: ChatCompletionsFunction,
    33→}
    34→
    35→/// The function definition nested inside ChatCompletionsTool.
    36→#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
    37→pub struct ChatCompletionsFunction {
    38→    pub name: String,
    39→    pub description: String,
    40→    pub parameters: serde_json::Value,
    41→    pub strict: bool,
    42→}
    43→
    44→/// Responses API tool format (flat, name at top level).
    45→///
    46→/// ```json
    47→/// { "type": "function", "name": "...", "parameters": {...}, "strict": true }
    48→/// ```
    49→#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
    50→pub struct ResponsesApiTool {
    51→    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    52→    pub tool_type: &'static str,
    53→    pub name: String,
    54→    pub description: String,
    55→    pub parameters: serde_json::Value,
    56→    pub strict: bool,
    57→}
    58→
    59→/// Make a JSON Schema node nullable by adding `"null"` to its type.
    60→///
    61→/// For schemas using `"type": "string"` (or any single type), this converts to
    62→/// `"type": ["string", "null"]`.  For schemas using `anyOf`/`oneOf`, it appends
    63→/// a `{"type": "null"}` variant.  Already-nullable schemas are left unchanged.
    64→fn make_nullable(schema: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    65→    let Some(obj) = schema.as_object_mut() else {
    66→        return;
    67→    };
    68→
    69→    // If it already has a "null" type, nothing to do.
    70→    if let Some(ty) = obj.get("type") {
    71→        if ty.as_str() == Some("null") {
    72→            return;
    73→        }
    74→        if let Some(arr) = ty.as_array()
    75→            && arr.iter().any(|v| v.as_str() == Some("null"))
    76→        {
    77→            return;
    78→        }
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    // Case 1: has a `type` field — convert to array form with "null".
    82→    if let Some(ty) = obj.get("type").cloned() {
    83→        if let Some(s) = ty.as_str() {
    84→            obj.insert("type".to_string(), serde_json::json!([s, "null"]));
    85→        } else if let Some(arr) = ty.as_array() {
    86→            let mut new_arr = arr.clone();
    87→            new_arr.push(serde_json::json!("null"));
    88→            obj.insert("type".to_string(), serde_json::Value::Array(new_arr));
    89→        }
    90→        return;
    91→    }
    92→
    93→    // Case 2: uses anyOf/oneOf — append a null variant.
    94→    for key in ["anyOf", "oneOf"] {
    95→        if let Some(variants) = obj.get_mut(key).and_then(|v| v.as_array_mut()) {
    96→            let has_null = variants
    97→                .iter()
    98→                .any(|v| v.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) == Some("null"));
    99→            if !has_null {
   100→                variants.push(serde_json::json!({"type": "null"}));
   101→            }
   102→            return;
   103→        }
   104→    }
   105→}
   106→
   107→/// Recursively patch schema for OpenAI strict mode compliance.
   108→///
   109→/// OpenAI's strict mode requires:
   110→/// 1. `additionalProperties: false` on every object in the schema tree
   111→/// 2. All properties must be listed in the `required` array
   112→///
   113→/// Properties not in the original `required` array are made nullable so the
   114→/// model can send `null` for unused optional fields instead of fabricating
   115→/// placeholder values.
   116→///
   117→/// This function recursively patches nested objects in `properties`, array
   118→/// `items`, `anyOf`/`oneOf`/`allOf` variants, etc.
   119→pub fn patch_schema_for_strict_mode(schema: &mut serde_json::Value) {
   120→    let Some(obj) = schema.as_object_mut() else {
   121→        return;
   122→    };
   123→
   124→    // Collect originally-optional property names so we can make them nullable
   125→    // AFTER recursion (otherwise changing "type":"object" to ["object","null"]
   126→    // would prevent the recursive pass from recognising nested objects).
   127→    let mut optional_props: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   128→
   129→    // If this is an object type, apply strict mode requirements
   130→    if obj.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) == Some("object") {
   131→        // Add additionalProperties: false
   132→        obj.insert("additionalProperties".to_string(), serde_json::json!(false));
   133→
   134→        // Ensure all properties are in required array.
   135→        if let Some(props) = obj.get("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object()).cloned() {
   136→            let originally_required: std::collections::HashSet<String> = obj
   137→                .get("required")
   138→                .and_then(|r| r.as_array())
   139→                .map(|arr| {
   140→                    arr.iter()
   141→                        .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(String::from))
   142→                        .collect()
   143→                })
   144→                .unwrap_or_default();
   145→
   146→            let all_prop_names: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   147→                props.keys().map(|k| serde_json::json!(k)).collect();
   148→            obj.insert("required".to_string(), serde_json::json!(all_prop_names));
   149→
   150→            // Remember which properties need to be made nullable (deferred).
   151→            for key in props.keys() {
   152→                if !originally_required.contains(key) {
   153→                    optional_props.push(key.clone());
   154→                }
   155→            }
   156→        } else {
   157→            // Object without properties - add empty properties and required
   158→            obj.insert("properties".to_string(), serde_json::json!({}));
   159→            obj.insert("required".to_string(), serde_json::json!([]));
   160→        }
   161→    }
   162→
   163→    // Recurse into properties
   164→    if let Some(props) = obj.get_mut("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object_mut()) {
   165→        for (_, prop_schema) in props.iter_mut() {
   166→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(prop_schema);
   167→        }
   168→    }
   169→
   170→    // Recurse into array items
   171→    if let Some(items) = obj.get_mut("items") {
   172→        patch_schema_for_strict_mode(items);
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    // Recurse into anyOf/oneOf/allOf
   176→    for key in ["anyOf", "oneOf", "allOf"] {
   177→        if let Some(variants) = obj.get_mut(key).and_then(|v| v.as_array_mut()) {
   178→            for variant in variants {
   179→                patch_schema_for_strict_mode(variant);
   180→            }
   181→        }
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    // Recurse into additionalProperties if it's a schema (not just true/false)
   185→    if let Some(additional) = obj.get_mut("additionalProperties")
   186→        && additional.is_object()
   187→    {
   188→        patch_schema_for_strict_mode(additional);
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    // Now that recursion is done, make originally-optional properties nullable
   192→    // so the model can send `null` instead of fabricating placeholder values
   193→    // (e.g. empty strings) that downstream MCP servers reject.
   194→    if !optional_props.is_empty()
   195→        && let Some(props) = obj.get_mut("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object_mut())
   196→    {
   197→        for key in &optional_props {
   198→            if let Some(prop_schema) = props.get_mut(key) {
   199→                make_nullable(prop_schema);
   200→            }
   201→        }
   202→    }
   203→}
   204→
   205→/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Chat Completions function-calling format.
   206→///
   207→/// Uses the nested `function` object format required by Chat Completions API:
   208→/// ```json
   209→/// { "type": "function", "function": { "name": "...", ... } }
   210→/// ```
   211→///
   212→/// Adds `strict: true` and patches schemas for strict mode compliance:
   213→/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas
   214→/// - All properties included in `required` array
   215→///
   216→/// This is required by some APIs (Claude via Copilot) to ensure the model
   217→/// provides all required fields.
   218→///
   219→/// See: <https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling>
   220→pub fn to_openai_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   221→    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools
   222→        .iter()
   223→        .filter_map(|t| {
   224→            // Clone parameters and patch for strict mode
   225→            let mut params = t["parameters"].clone();
   226→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);
   227→
   228→            let name = t["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   229→            let description = t["description"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   230→
   231→            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present
   232→            let tool = ChatCompletionsTool {
   233→                tool_type: "function",
   234→                function: ChatCompletionsFunction {
   235→                    name: name.clone(),
   236→                    description,
   237→                    parameters: params,
   238→                    strict: true,
   239→                },
   240→            };
   241→
   242→            trace!(tool_name = %name, "converted tool to Chat Completions format");
   243→
   244→            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API
   245→            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()
   246→        })
   247→        .collect();
   248→
   249→    trace!(tools_count = result.len(), "to_openai_tools complete");
   250→    result
   251→}
   252→
   253→/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Responses API function-calling format.
   254→///
   255→/// Uses the flat format required by the Responses API where `name` is at top level:
   256→/// ```json
   257→/// { "type": "function", "name": "...", "parameters": {...}, "strict": true }
   258→/// ```
   259→///
   260→/// This is the format used by OpenAI Codex and the Responses API.
   261→///
   262→/// Patches schemas for strict mode compliance:
   263→/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas
   264→/// - All properties included in `required` array
   265→///
   266→/// See: <https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-foundry/openai/how-to/responses>
   267→pub fn to_responses_api_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   268→    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools
   269→        .iter()
   270→        .filter_map(|t| {
   271→            // Clone parameters and patch for strict mode
   272→            let mut params = t["parameters"].clone();
   273→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);
   274→
   275→            let name = t["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   276→            let description = t["description"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   277→
   278→            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present
   279→            let tool = ResponsesApiTool {
   280→                tool_type: "function",
   281→                name: name.clone(),
   282→                description,
   283→                parameters: params,
   284→                strict: true,
   285→            };
   286→
   287→            trace!(tool_name = %name, "converted tool to Responses API format");
   288→
   289→            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API
   290→            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()
   291→        })
   292→        .collect();
   293→
   294→    trace!(
   295→        tools_count = result.len(),
   296→        "to_responses_api_tools complete"
   297→    );
   298→    result
   299→}
   300→
   301→/// Convert typed chat messages to Responses API input items.
   302→///
   303→/// Responses API accepts a heterogeneous input array (messages, tool calls, and
   304→/// tool outputs). This keeps one canonical conversion for providers that use
   305→/// Responses transport (SSE or WebSocket).
   306→#[must_use]
   307→pub fn to_responses_input(messages: &[ChatMessage]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   308→    messages
   309→        .iter()
   310→        .flat_map(|msg| match msg {
   311→            ChatMessage::System { .. } => {
   312→                // System messages are extracted into `instructions`.
   313→                vec![]
   314→            },
   315→            ChatMessage::User { content } => {
   316→                let content_blocks = match content {
   317→                    UserContent::Text(t) => {
   318→                        vec![serde_json::json!({"type": "input_text", "text": t})]
   319→                    },
   320→                    UserContent::Multimodal(parts) => parts
   321→                        .iter()
   322→                        .map(|p| match p {
   323→                            moltis_agents::model::ContentPart::Text(t) => {
   324→                                serde_json::json!({"type": "input_text", "text": t})
   325→                            },
   326→                            moltis_agents::model::ContentPart::Image { media_type, data } => {
   327→                                let data_uri = format!("data:{media_type};base64,{data}");
   328→                                serde_json::json!({
   329→                                    "type": "input_image",
   330→                                    "image_url": data_uri,
   331→                                })
   332→                            },
   333→                        })
   334→                        .collect(),
   335→                };
   336→                vec![serde_json::json!({
   337→                    "role": "user",
   338→                    "content": content_blocks,
   339→                })]
   340→            },
   341→            ChatMessage::Assistant {
   342→                content,
   343→                tool_calls,
   344→            } => {
   345→                if !tool_calls.is_empty() {
   346→                    let mut items: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tool_calls
   347→                        .iter()
   348→                        .map(|tc| {
   349→                            serde_json::json!({
   350→                                "type": "function_call",
   351→                                "call_id": tc.id,
   352→                                "name": tc.name,
   353→                                "arguments": tc.arguments.to_string(),
   354→                            })
   355→                        })
   356→                        .collect();
   357→                    if let Some(text) = content
   358→                        && !text.is_empty()
   359→                    {
   360→                        items.insert(
   361→                            0,
   362→                            serde_json::json!({
   363→                                "type": "message",
   364→                                "role": "assistant",
   365→                                "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": text}]
   366→                            }),
   367→                        );
   368→                    }
   369→                    items
   370→                } else {
   371→                    let text = content.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
   372→                    vec![serde_json::json!({
   373→                        "type": "message",
   374→                        "role": "assistant",
   375→                        "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": text}]
   376→                    })]
   377→                }
   378→            },
   379→            ChatMessage::Tool {
   380→                tool_call_id,
   381→                content,
   382→            } => {
   383→                vec![serde_json::json!({
   384→                    "type": "function_call_output",
   385→                    "call_id": tool_call_id,
   386→                    "output": content,
   387→                })]
   388→            },
   389→        })
   390→        .collect()
   391→}
   392→
   393→/// Parse tool_calls from an OpenAI response message (non-streaming).
   394→pub fn parse_tool_calls(message: &serde_json::Value) -> Vec<ToolCall> {
   395→    message["tool_calls"]
   396→        .as_array()
   397→        .map(|tcs| {
   398→            tcs.iter()
   399→                .filter_map(|tc| {
   400→                    let id = tc["id"].as_str()?.to_string();
   401→                    let name = tc["function"]["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   402→                    let args_str = tc["function"]["arguments"].as_str().unwrap_or("{}");
   403→                    let arguments = serde_json::from_str(args_str).unwrap_or(serde_json::json!({}));
   404→                    Some(ToolCall {
   405→                        id,
   406→                        name,
   407→                        arguments,
   408→                    })
   409→                })
   410→                .collect()
   411→        })
   412→        .unwrap_or_default()
   413→}
   414→
   415→fn usage_value_at_path(usage: &serde_json::Value, path: &[&str]) -> Option<u64> {
   416→    let mut cursor = usage;
   417→    for key in path {
   418→        cursor = cursor.get(*key)?;
   419→    }
   420→    cursor
   421→        .as_u64()
   422→        .or_else(|| cursor.as_str().and_then(|raw| raw.parse::<u64>().ok()))
   423→}
   424→
   425→fn usage_field_u32(usage: &serde_json::Value, paths: &[&[&str]]) -> u32 {
   426→    paths
   427→        .iter()
   428→        .find_map(|path| usage_value_at_path(usage, path))
   429→        .unwrap_or(0) as u32
   430→}
   431→
   432→fn usage_object_from_payload(payload: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<&serde_json::Value> {
   433→    if let Some(usage) = payload.get("usage").filter(|usage| usage.is_object()) {
   434→        return Some(usage);
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   438→        .get("choices")
   439→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   440→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   441→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("usage"))
   442→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   443→    {
   444→        return Some(usage);
   445→    }
   446→
   447→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   448→        .get("choices")
   449→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   450→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   451→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("delta"))
   452→        .and_then(|delta| delta.get("usage"))
   453→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   454→    {
   455→        return Some(usage);
   456→    }
   457→
   458→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   459→        .get("choices")
   460→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   461→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   462→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("message"))
   463→        .and_then(|message| message.get("usage"))
   464→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   465→    {
   466→        return Some(usage);
   467→    }
   468→
   469→    payload
   470→        .get("x_groq")
   471→        .and_then(|x_groq| x_groq.get("usage"))
   472→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   473→}
   474→
   475→/// Parse usage payloads from OpenAI-compatible backends.
   476→///
   477→/// Different providers use different field names:
   478→/// - OpenAI-style: `prompt_tokens`, `completion_tokens`
   479→/// - Anthropic/MiniMax-style: `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`
   480→/// - Cache fields may be top-level or nested in `*_tokens_details`.
   481→#[must_use]
   482→pub fn parse_openai_compat_usage(usage: &serde_json::Value) -> Usage {
   483→    Usage {
   484→        input_tokens: usage_field_u32(usage, &[
   485→            &["prompt_tokens"],
   486→            &["promptTokens"],
   487→            &["input_tokens"],
   488→            &["inputTokens"],
   489→        ]),
   490→        output_tokens: usage_field_u32(usage, &[
   491→            &["completion_tokens"],
   492→            &["completionTokens"],
   493→            &["output_tokens"],
   494→            &["outputTokens"],
   495→        ]),
   496→        cache_read_tokens: usage_field_u32(usage, &[
   497→            &["prompt_tokens_details", "cached_tokens"],
   498→            &["promptTokensDetails", "cachedTokens"],
   499→            &["cache_read_input_tokens"],
   500→            &["cacheReadInputTokens"],
```

   513→/// Parse usage from an OpenAI-compatible payload, checking common nesting variants.
   514→///
   515→/// Providers differ on where they place usage metadata:
   516→/// - top-level `usage`
   517→/// - `choices[0].usage`
   518→/// - `choices[0].delta.usage`
   519→/// - `choices[0].message.usage`
   520→/// - provider extension blocks (for example `x_groq.usage`)
   521→#[must_use]
   522→pub fn parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(payload: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<Usage> {
   523→    usage_object_from_payload(payload).map(parse_openai_compat_usage)
   524→}
   525→
   526→/// Strip `<think>...</think>` tags from content, returning `(visible, thinking)`.
   527→///
   528→/// Models like DeepSeek R1, QwQ, and MiniMax embed chain-of-thought reasoning
   529→/// inside `<think>` tags in the `content` field rather than using a separate
   530→/// `reasoning_content` field.  This helper splits content into the visible
   531→/// answer text and the thinking text so callers can handle them appropriately.
   532→///
   533→/// Edge cases handled:
   534→/// - Multiple `<think>` blocks interspersed with answer text
   535→/// - Unclosed `<think>` tag (remainder treated as reasoning)
   536→/// - Empty `<think></think>` blocks
   537→/// - Nested angle brackets inside thinking text
   538→pub fn strip_think_tags(content: &str) -> (String, String) {
   539→    let mut visible = String::new();
   540→    let mut thinking = String::new();
   541→    let mut remaining = content;
   542→
   543→    loop {
   544→        match remaining.find("<think>") {
   545→            Some(start) => {
   546→                // Text before <think> is visible
   547→                visible.push_str(&remaining[..start]);
   548→                let after_open = &remaining[start + "<think>".len()..];
   549→                match after_open.find("</think>") {
   550→                    Some(end) => {
   551→                        thinking.push_str(&after_open[..end]);
   552→                        remaining = &after_open[end + "</think>".len()..];
   553→                    },
   554→                    None => {
   555→                        // Unclosed <think> — treat rest as reasoning
   556→                        thinking.push_str(after_open);
   557→                        break;
   558→                    },
   559→                }
   560→            },
   561→            None => {
   562→                visible.push_str(remaining);
   563→                break;
   564→            },
   565→        }
   566→    }
   567→
   568→    (
   569→        visible.trim_start().to_string(),
   570→        thinking.trim_start().to_string(),
   571→    )
   572→}
   573→
   574→/// State for tracking streaming tool calls.
   575→#[derive(Default)]
   576→pub struct StreamingToolState {
   577→    /// Map from index -> (id, name, arguments_buffer)
   578→    pub tool_calls: HashMap<usize, (String, String, String)>,
   579→    pub input_tokens: u32,
   580→    pub output_tokens: u32,
   581→    pub cache_read_tokens: u32,
   582→    pub cache_write_tokens: u32,
   583→    /// Whether we are currently inside a `<think>` block in streamed content.
   584→    in_think_block: bool,
   585→    /// Whether we are still stripping leading whitespace at the start of a
   586→    /// think block. Set to `true` when entering `<think>`, cleared once
   587→    /// non-whitespace reasoning content is emitted.
   588→    think_strip_leading_ws: bool,
   589→    /// Whether we are still stripping leading whitespace from visible content
   590→    /// after exiting a `</think>` block. Models often emit `\n\n` between
   591→    /// `</think>` and the actual answer.
   592→    visible_strip_leading_ws: bool,
   593→    /// Buffer for detecting `<think>` / `</think>` tags that may be split
   594→    /// across SSE chunk boundaries.
   595→    tag_buffer: String,
   596→}
   597→
   598→/// Result of processing a single SSE line.
   599→pub enum SseLineResult {
   600→    /// No actionable event (empty line, non-data prefix)
   601→    Skip,
   602→    /// Stream is done
   603→    Done,
   604→    /// Events to yield
   605→    Events(Vec<StreamEvent>),
   606→}
   607→
   608→/// Emit a `ReasoningDelta`, stripping leading whitespace at the start of a
   609→/// think block so the UI doesn't show a blank prefix.
   610→fn emit_reasoning(text: String, strip_leading_ws: &mut bool, events: &mut Vec<StreamEvent>) {
   611→    if text.is_empty() {
   612→        return;
   613→    }
   614→    let emitted = if *strip_leading_ws {
   615→        let trimmed = text.trim_start();
   616→        if trimmed.is_empty() {
   617→            // Entire chunk was whitespace — keep stripping
   618→            return;
   619→        }
   620→        *strip_leading_ws = false;
   621→        trimmed.to_string()
   622→    } else {
   623→        text
   624→    };
   625→    events.push(StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(emitted));
   626→}
   627→
   628→/// Emit a visible `Delta`, stripping leading whitespace after a `</think>`
   629→/// block so the UI doesn't show blank lines before the answer.
   630→fn emit_visible(text: String, strip_leading_ws: &mut bool, events: &mut Vec<StreamEvent>) {
   631→    if text.is_empty() {
   632→        return;
   633→    }
   634→    let emitted = if *strip_leading_ws {
   635→        let trimmed = text.trim_start();
   636→        if trimmed.is_empty() {
   637→            // Entire chunk was whitespace — keep stripping
   638→            return;
   639→        }
   640→        *strip_leading_ws = false;
   641→        trimmed.to_string()
   642→    } else {
   643→        text
   644→    };
   645→    events.push(StreamEvent::Delta(emitted));
   646→}
   647→
   648→/// Process streamed content through the `<think>` tag state machine.
   649→///
   650→/// Content arriving inside `<think>...</think>` is emitted as
   651→/// `ReasoningDelta`; content outside is emitted as `Delta`.
   652→/// Tags may be split across SSE chunks — `tag_buffer` accumulates
   653→/// partial tag fragments until they can be resolved.
   654→/// Leading whitespace at the start of each think block is stripped.
   655→fn process_content_think_tags(
   656→    content: &str,
   657→    state: &mut StreamingToolState,
   658→    events: &mut Vec<StreamEvent>,
   659→) {
   660→    state.tag_buffer.push_str(content);
   661→
   662→    loop {
   663→        if state.in_think_block {
   664→            // Look for </think> to exit think mode
   665→            match state.tag_buffer.find("</think>") {
   666→                Some(pos) => {
   667→                    let thinking = state.tag_buffer[..pos].to_string();
   668→                    emit_reasoning(thinking, &mut state.think_strip_leading_ws, events);
   669→                    state.in_think_block = false;
   670→                    state.visible_strip_leading_ws = true;
   671→                    let rest = state.tag_buffer[pos + "</think>".len()..].to_string();
   672→                    state.tag_buffer = rest;
   673→                    // Continue loop to process remaining content
   674→                },
   675→                None => {
   676→                    // Check if buffer ends with a prefix of "</think>"
   677→                    // to avoid emitting partial tag as reasoning text.
   678→                    let suffix_match = longest_tag_suffix(&state.tag_buffer, "</think>");
   679→                    if suffix_match > 0 {
   680→                        let safe = state.tag_buffer.len() - suffix_match;
   681→                        let emit = state.tag_buffer[..safe].to_string();
   682→                        emit_reasoning(emit, &mut state.think_strip_leading_ws, events);
   683→                        let kept = state.tag_buffer[safe..].to_string();
   684→                        state.tag_buffer = kept;
   685→                    } else {
   686→                        // No partial tag — emit everything as reasoning
   687→                        let buf = std::mem::take(&mut state.tag_buffer);
   688→                        emit_reasoning(buf, &mut state.think_strip_leading_ws, events);
   689→                    }
   690→                    break;
   691→                },
   692→            }
   693→        } else {
   694→            // Look for <think> to enter think mode
   695→            match state.tag_buffer.find("<think>") {
   696→                Some(pos) => {
   697→                    let visible = state.tag_buffer[..pos].to_string();
   698→                    emit_visible(visible, &mut state.visible_strip_leading_ws, events);
   699→                    state.in_think_block = true;
   700→                    state.think_strip_leading_ws = true;
   701→                    let rest = state.tag_buffer[pos + "<think>".len()..].to_string();
   702→                    state.tag_buffer = rest;
   703→                    // Continue loop to process remaining content
   704→                },
   705→                None => {
   706→                    // Check if buffer ends with a prefix of "<think>"
   707→                    let suffix_match = longest_tag_suffix(&state.tag_buffer, "<think>");
   708→                    if suffix_match > 0 {
   709→                        let safe = state.tag_buffer.len() - suffix_match;
   710→                        let emit = state.tag_buffer[..safe].to_string();
   711→                        emit_visible(emit, &mut state.visible_strip_leading_ws, events);
   712→                        let kept = state.tag_buffer[safe..].to_string();
   713→                        state.tag_buffer = kept;
   714→                    } else {
   715→                        // No partial tag — emit everything as visible
   716→                        let buf = std::mem::take(&mut state.tag_buffer);
   717→                        emit_visible(buf, &mut state.visible_strip_leading_ws, events);
   718→                    }
   719→                    break;
   720→                },
   721→            }
   722→        }
   723→    }
   724→}
   725→
   726→/// Return the length of the longest suffix of `text` that is a prefix of `tag`.
   727→///
   728→/// For example, `longest_tag_suffix("abc<th", "<think>")` returns 3 because
   729→/// `"<th"` is a 3-character prefix of `"<think>"`.
   730→fn longest_tag_suffix(text: &str, tag: &str) -> usize {
   731→    let text_bytes = text.as_bytes();
   732→    let tag_bytes = tag.as_bytes();
   733→    let max_check = text_bytes.len().min(tag_bytes.len());
   734→    for len in (1..=max_check).rev() {
   735→        if text_bytes[text_bytes.len() - len..] == tag_bytes[..len] {
   736→            return len;
   737→        }
   738→    }
   739→    0
   740→}
   741→
   742→/// Process a single SSE data line and return any events to yield.
   743→///
   744→/// This handles the common OpenAI streaming format used by:
   745→/// - OpenAI API
   746→/// - GitHub Copilot API
   747→/// - Kimi Code API
   748→/// - Any other OpenAI-compatible API
   749→///
   750→/// Content inside `<think>...</think>` tags is emitted as `ReasoningDelta`
   751→/// events rather than `Delta`, allowing the UI to show reasoning text
   752→/// separately. This handles models (DeepSeek R1, QwQ, MiniMax) that embed
   753→/// chain-of-thought in `content` rather than using `reasoning_content`.
   754→pub fn process_openai_sse_line(data: &str, state: &mut StreamingToolState) -> SseLineResult {
   755→    if data == "[DONE]" {
   756→        return SseLineResult::Done;
   757→    }
   758→
   759→    let Ok(evt) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(data) else {
   760→        return SseLineResult::Skip;
   761→    };
   762→
   763→    let mut events = vec![StreamEvent::ProviderRaw(evt.clone())];
   764→
   765→    if let Some(usage) = parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(&evt) {
   766→        state.input_tokens = usage.input_tokens;
   767→        state.output_tokens = usage.output_tokens;
   768→        state.cache_read_tokens = usage.cache_read_tokens;
   769→        state.cache_write_tokens = usage.cache_write_tokens;
   770→    }
   771→
   772→    let delta = &evt["choices"][0]["delta"];
   773→
   774→    // Handle user-visible text content, stripping <think> tags.
   775→    if let Some(content) = delta["content"].as_str()
   776→        && !content.is_empty()
   777→    {
   778→        process_content_think_tags(content, state, &mut events);
   779→    }
   780→
   781→    // Some OpenAI-compatible backends stream planning text in
   782→    // `reasoning_content` or `reasoning`. Surface it separately so UI can
   783→    //  show it in the thinking area without polluting final assistant text.
   784→    let reasoning_text = delta["reasoning_content"]
   785→        .as_str()
   786→        .or_else(|| delta["reasoning"].as_str());
   787→    if let Some(reasoning_content) = reasoning_text
   788→        && !reasoning_content.is_empty()
   789→    {
   790→        events.push(StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(reasoning_content.to_string()));
   791→    }
   792→
   793→    // Handle tool calls
   794→    if let Some(tcs) = delta["tool_calls"].as_array() {
   795→        for tc in tcs {
   796→            let index = tc["index"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize;
   797→
   798→            // Check if this is a new tool call (has id and function.name)
   799→            if let (Some(id), Some(name)) = (tc["id"].as_str(), tc["function"]["name"].as_str()) {
   800→                state
   801→                    .tool_calls
   802→                    .insert(index, (id.to_string(), name.to_string(), String::new()));
   803→                events.push(StreamEvent::ToolCallStart {
   804→                    id: id.to_string(),
   805→                    name: name.to_string(),
   806→                    index,
   807→                });
   808→            }
   809→
   810→            // Handle arguments delta
   811→            if let Some(args_delta) = tc["function"]["arguments"].as_str()
   812→                && !args_delta.is_empty()
   813→            {
   814→                if let Some((_, _, args_buf)) = state.tool_calls.get_mut(&index) {
   815→                    args_buf.push_str(args_delta);
   816→                }
   817→                events.push(StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta {
   818→                    index,
   819→                    delta: args_delta.to_string(),
   820→                });
   821→            }
   822→        }
   823→    }
   824→
   825→    // Detect error finish reasons (e.g. "network_error", "content_filter").
   826→    // Normal reasons (null, "stop", "tool_calls", "length") are not errors.
   827→    if let Some(reason) = evt["choices"][0]["finish_reason"].as_str() {
   828→        match reason {
   829→            "stop" | "tool_calls" | "length" | "function_call" => {},
   830→            error_reason => {
   831→                events.push(StreamEvent::Error(format!(
   832→                    "Provider stream ended with finish_reason: {error_reason}"
   833→                )));
   834→            },
   835→        }
   836→    }
   837→
   838→    SseLineResult::Events(events)
   839→}
   840→
   841→/// Generate the final events when stream ends (tool call completions + done).
   842→///
   843→/// Any residual content in the think-tag buffer is flushed as the appropriate
   844→/// event type (reasoning if we were inside a think block, visible otherwise).
   845→pub fn finalize_stream(state: &mut StreamingToolState) -> Vec<StreamEvent> {
   846→    let mut events = Vec::new();
   847→
   848→    // Flush any remaining think-tag buffer content
   849→    if !state.tag_buffer.is_empty() {
   850→        let remaining = std::mem::take(&mut state.tag_buffer);
   851→        if state.in_think_block {
   852→            events.push(StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(remaining));
   853→        } else {
   854→            events.push(StreamEvent::Delta(remaining));
   855→        }
   856→    }
   857→
   858→    // Emit completion for any pending tool calls
   859→    for index in state.tool_calls.keys() {
   860→        events.push(StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete { index: *index });
   861→    }
   862→
   863→    events.push(StreamEvent::Done(Usage {
   864→        input_tokens: state.input_tokens,
   865→        output_tokens: state.output_tokens,
   866→        cache_read_tokens: state.cache_read_tokens,
   867→        cache_write_tokens: state.cache_write_tokens,
   868→    }));
   869→
   870→    events
   871→}
   872→
   873→#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]

   917→                }

   972→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({

   987→        let converted = to_openai_tools(&tools);
   988→        let params = &converted[0]["function"]["parameters"];
   989→
   990→        // The object variant in anyOf should have additionalProperties: false
   991→        let any_of = params["properties"]["value"]["anyOf"].as_array().unwrap();
   992→        // First variant is string, no additionalProperties needed
   993→        // Second variant is object, should have additionalProperties: false
   994→        assert_eq!(any_of[1]["additionalProperties"], false);
   995→
   996→        // "value" is not in original required, so a null variant is appended
   997→        assert_eq!(any_of.len(), 3);
   998→        assert_eq!(any_of[2], serde_json::json!({"type": "null"}));
   999→    }
