  1001→    #[test]
  1002→    fn test_to_openai_tools_all_properties_required_and_optional_nullable() {
  1003→        // All properties are in `required`, but originally-optional ones
  1004→        // become nullable so the model can send null instead of empty strings.
  1005→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({
  1006→            "name": "web_fetch",
  1007→            "description": "Fetch a URL",
  1008→            "parameters": {
  1009→                "type": "object",
  1010→                "properties": {
  1011→                    "url": {"type": "string"},
  1012→                    "extract_mode": {"type": "string", "enum": ["markdown", "text"]},
  1013→                    "max_chars": {"type": "integer"}
  1014→                },
  1015→                "required": ["url"]  // Only url was originally required
  1016→            }
  1017→        })];
  1018→        let converted = to_openai_tools(&tools);
  1019→        let params = &converted[0]["function"]["parameters"];
  1020→
  1021→        // All properties should be in required array
  1022→        let required = params["required"].as_array().unwrap();
  1023→        assert_eq!(required.len(), 3);
  1024→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("url")));
  1025→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("extract_mode")));
  1026→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("max_chars")));
  1027→
  1028→        // Originally-required "url" keeps its original type
  1029→        assert_eq!(params["properties"]["url"]["type"], "string");
  1030→
  1031→        // Originally-optional properties become nullable
  1032→        let em_type = params["properties"]["extract_mode"]["type"]
  1033→            .as_array()
  1034→            .unwrap();
  1035→        assert!(em_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("string")));
  1036→        assert!(em_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("null")));
  1037→
  1038→        let mc_type = params["properties"]["max_chars"]["type"]
  1039→            .as_array()
  1040→            .unwrap();
  1041→        assert!(mc_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("integer")));
  1042→        assert!(mc_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("null")));
  1043→    }

  1361→    #[test]
  1362→    fn test_finalize_stream() {
  1363→        let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
  1364→        state
  1365→            .tool_calls
  1366→            .insert(0, ("call_1".into(), "test".into(), "{}".into()));

  1369→
  1370→        let events = finalize_stream(&mut state);
  1371→        assert_eq!(events.len(), 2);
  1372→        assert!(matches!(&events[0], StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete {
  1373→            index: 0
  1374→        }));
  1375→        assert!(matches!(
  1376→            &events[1],
  1377→            StreamEvent::Done(usage) if usage.input_tokens == 10 && usage.output_tokens == 5
  1378→        ));
  1379→    }
  1380→
  1381→    // ============================================================
  1382→    // Tests for to_responses_api_tools (OpenAI Responses API format)
  1383→    // See: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-foundry/openai/how-to/responses
  1384→    // ============================================================
  1385→
  1386→    #[test]
  1387→    fn test_to_responses_api_tools_format() {
  1388→        // Responses API uses flat format: name at top level, not nested under "function"
  1389→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({
  1390→            "name": "get_weather",
  1391→            "description": "Get the weather for a location",
  1392→            "parameters": {
  1393→                "type": "object",
  1394→                "properties": {
  1395→                    "location": {"type": "string"}
  1396→                },
  1397→                "required": ["location"]
  1398→            }
  1399→        })];
  1400→        let converted = to_responses_api_tools(&tools);
  1401→        assert_eq!(converted.len(), 1);
  1402→
  1403→        // Verify flat format (name at top level, not nested under function)
  1404→        assert_eq!(converted[0]["type"], "function");
  1405→        assert_eq!(converted[0]["name"], "get_weather");
  1406→        assert_eq!(
  1407→            converted[0]["description"],
  1408→            "Get the weather for a location"
  1409→        );
  1410→        assert_eq!(converted[0]["strict"], true);
  1411→
  1412→        // Should NOT have a "function" wrapper
  1413→        assert!(converted[0].get("function").is_none());
  1414→
  1415→        // Parameters should be patched for strict mode
  1416→        assert_eq!(converted[0]["parameters"]["additionalProperties"], false);
  1417→    }
  1418→
  1419→    #[test]
  1420→    fn test_to_responses_api_tools_nested_objects() {
  1421→        // Test that nested objects get additionalProperties: false and required
  1422→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({
  1423→            "name": "delete_observations",
  1424→            "description": "Delete observations",
  1425→            "parameters": {
  1426→                "type": "object",
  1427→                "properties": {
  1428→                    "deletions": {
  1429→                        "type": "array",
  1430→                        "items": {
  1431→                            "type": "object",
  1432→                            "properties": {
  1433→                                "observation": {"type": "string"},
  1434→                                "entity": {"type": "string"}
  1435→                            }
  1436→                        }
  1437→                    }
  1438→                }
  1439→            }
  1440→        })];
  1441→        let converted = to_responses_api_tools(&tools);
  1442→        let params = &converted[0]["parameters"];
  1443→
  1444→        // Top level should have additionalProperties: false
  1445→        assert_eq!(params["additionalProperties"], false);
  1446→
  1447→        // All properties at top level should be in required
  1448→        let required = params["required"].as_array().unwrap();
  1449→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("deletions")));
  1450→
  1451→        // Array items object should have additionalProperties: false
  1452→        let items = &params["properties"]["deletions"]["items"];
  1453→        assert_eq!(items["additionalProperties"], false);
  1454→
  1455→        // Array items should have all properties in required
  1456→        let items_required = items["required"].as_array().unwrap();
  1457→        assert!(items_required.contains(&serde_json::json!("observation")));
  1458→        assert!(items_required.contains(&serde_json::json!("entity")));
  1459→    }
  1460→
  1461→    #[test]
  1462→    fn test_object_without_properties() {
  1463→        // Objects without explicit properties need empty properties + empty required
  1464→        // This was failing for the cron tool's "patch" field
  1465→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({
  1466→            "name": "cron",
  1467→            "description": "Cron tool",
  1468→            "parameters": {
  1469→                "type": "object",
  1470→                "properties": {
  1471→                    "action": {"type": "string"},
  1472→                    "patch": {
  1473→                        "type": "object",
  1474→                        "description": "Fields to update (no properties defined)"
  1475→                    }
  1476→                },
  1477→                "required": ["action"]
  1478→            }
  1479→        })];
  1480→        let converted = to_responses_api_tools(&tools);
  1481→        let params = &converted[0]["parameters"];
  1482→
  1483→        // Top level should have all properties in required
  1484→        let required = params["required"].as_array().unwrap();
  1485→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("action")));
  1486→        assert!(required.contains(&serde_json::json!("patch")));
  1487→
  1488→        // The "patch" object should have empty properties and empty required.
  1489→        // Because "patch" was not in the original `required`, it is also nullable.
  1490→        let patch = &params["properties"]["patch"];
  1491→        let patch_type = patch["type"].as_array().unwrap();
  1492→        assert!(patch_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("object")));
  1493→        assert!(patch_type.contains(&serde_json::json!("null")));
  1494→        assert_eq!(patch["additionalProperties"], false);
  1495→        assert_eq!(patch["properties"], serde_json::json!({}));
  1496→        assert_eq!(patch["required"], serde_json::json!([]));
  1497→    }
  1498→
  1499→    #[test]
  1500→    fn test_chat_completions_vs_responses_api_format() {
  1501→        // Verify the two formats are different
  1502→        let tools = vec![serde_json::json!({
  1503→            "name": "test_tool",
  1504→            "description": "A test tool",
  1505→            "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {"x": {"type": "string"}}}
  1506→        })];
  1507→
  1508→        let chat_completions = to_openai_tools(&tools);
  1509→        let responses_api = to_responses_api_tools(&tools);
  1510→
  1511→        // Chat Completions: nested under "function"
  1512→        assert!(chat_completions[0].get("function").is_some());
  1513→        assert_eq!(chat_completions[0]["function"]["name"], "test_tool");
  1514→
  1515→        // Responses API: flat format
  1516→        assert!(responses_api[0].get("function").is_none());
  1517→        assert_eq!(responses_api[0]["name"], "test_tool");
  1518→    }

  1599→    #[test]
  1600→    fn test_stream_think_block_single_chunk() {
  1601→        let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
  1602→        // Full think block + answer in one SSE chunk
  1603→        let data = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"<think>reasoning</think>The answer"}}]}"#;
  1604→        let result = process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state);
  1605→        match result {
  1606→            SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
  1607→                let reasoning: Vec<_> = events
  1608→                    .iter()
  1609→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1610→                        StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(s) => Some(s.as_str()),
  1611→                        _ => None,
  1612→                    })
  1613→                    .collect();
  1614→                let visible: Vec<_> = events
  1615→                    .iter()
  1616→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1617→                        StreamEvent::Delta(s) => Some(s.as_str()),
  1618→                        _ => None,
  1619→                    })
  1620→                    .collect();
  1621→                assert_eq!(reasoning.join(""), "reasoning");
  1622→                assert_eq!(visible.join(""), "The answer");
  1623→            },
  1624→            _ => panic!("Expected Events"),
  1625→        }
  1626→    }
  1627→
  1628→    #[test]
  1629→    fn test_stream_think_tag_split_across_chunks() {
  1630→        let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
  1631→
  1632→        // First chunk: starts with partial opening tag
  1633→        let data1 = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"<thi"}}]}"#;
  1634→        let result1 = process_openai_sse_line(data1, &mut state);
  1635→        // Should hold back the partial tag — only ProviderRaw emitted, no Delta
  1636→        match result1 {
  1637→            SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
  1638→                let delta_count = events
  1639→                    .iter()
  1640→                    .filter(|e| matches!(e, StreamEvent::Delta(_)))
  1641→                    .count();
  1642→                assert_eq!(delta_count, 0, "partial tag should be buffered");
  1643→            },
  1644→            SseLineResult::Skip => {},
  1645→            _ => panic!("Expected Events or Skip"),
  1646→        }
  1647→
  1648→        // Second chunk: completes the opening tag + reasoning
  1649→        let data2 = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"nk>deep thought</think>42"}}]}"#;
  1650→        let result2 = process_openai_sse_line(data2, &mut state);
  1651→        match result2 {
  1652→            SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
  1653→                let reasoning: String = events
  1654→                    .iter()
  1655→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1656→                        StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(s) => Some(s.as_str()),
  1657→                        _ => None,
  1658→                    })
  1659→                    .collect();
  1660→                let visible: String = events
  1661→                    .iter()
  1662→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1663→                        StreamEvent::Delta(s) => Some(s.as_str()),
  1664→                        _ => None,
  1665→                    })
  1666→                    .collect();
  1667→                assert_eq!(reasoning, "deep thought");
  1668→                assert_eq!(visible, "42");
  1669→            },
  1670→            _ => panic!("Expected Events"),
  1671→        }
  1672→    }

  1704→    fn test_stream_unclosed_think_emits_as_reasoning() {

  1713→        match result {

  1717→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {

  1720→                    })
  1721→                    .collect();

  1728→    #[test]
  1729→    fn test_stream_unclosed_think_partial_tag_flushed_on_finalize() {
  1730→        let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
  1731→
  1732→        // Enter think mode, then receive content ending with a partial </think>
  1733→        let data1 = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"<think>start"}}]}"#;
  1734→        let _ = process_openai_sse_line(data1, &mut state);
  1735→        assert!(state.in_think_block);
  1736→
  1737→        // Content ending with partial closing tag
  1738→        let data2 = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":" more</thi"}}]}"#;
  1739→        let _ = process_openai_sse_line(data2, &mut state);
  1740→        // The "</thi" should be buffered as a partial tag
  1741→
  1742→        // Finalize should flush the buffered partial tag as reasoning
  1743→        let final_events = finalize_stream(&mut state);
  1744→        let has_reasoning = final_events
  1745→            .iter()
  1746→            .any(|e| matches!(e, StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(s) if s.contains("</thi")));
  1747→        assert!(
  1748→            has_reasoning,
  1749→            "finalize should flush partial tag buffer as reasoning"
  1750→        );
  1751→    }

  1753→    #[test]
  1754→    fn test_stream_think_coexists_with_reasoning_content() {
  1755→        // Models that send both reasoning_content AND <think> tags
  1756→        let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
  1757→        let data = r#"{"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"<think>inline thought</think>answer","reasoning_content":"separate reasoning"}}]}"#;
  1758→        let result = process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state);
  1759→        match result {
  1760→            SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
  1761→                let reasoning_deltas: Vec<_> = events
  1762→                    .iter()
  1763→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1764→                        StreamEvent::ReasoningDelta(s) => Some(s.clone()),
  1765→                        _ => None,
  1766→                    })
  1767→                    .collect();
  1768→                let visible: String = events
  1769→                    .iter()
  1770→                    .filter_map(|e| match e {
  1771→                        StreamEvent::Delta(s) => Some(s.as_str()),
  1772→                        _ => None,
  1773→                    })
  1774→                    .collect();
  1775→                // Both reasoning sources should produce ReasoningDelta events
  1776→                assert!(reasoning_deltas.contains(&"inline thought".to_string()));
  1777→                assert!(reasoning_deltas.contains(&"separate reasoning".to_string()));
  1778→                assert_eq!(visible, "answer");
  1779→            },
  1780→            _ => panic!("Expected Events"),
  1781→        }
  1782→    }

  1797→        }
  1798→    }

  1804→        // Enter think mode

  1818→                        _ => None,
  1819→                    })
  1820→                    .collect();
  1821→                assert_eq!(visible, "visible");
  1822→                assert!(!state.in_think_block);
  1823→            },
  1824→            _ => panic!("Expected Events"),
  1825→        }

  1902→    #[test]

   335→fn ws_output_index(event: &serde_json::Value, fallback: usize) -> usize {
   336→    event
   337→        .get("output_index")
   338→        .or_else(|| event.get("item_index"))
   339→        .or_else(|| event.get("index"))
   340→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_u64)
   341→        .map(|i| i as usize)
   342→        .unwrap_or(fallback)
   343→}

   345→async fn fetch_models_from_api(
   346→    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
   347→    base_url: String,
   348→) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   349→    let client = crate::shared_http_client();
   350→    let response = client
   351→        .get(models_endpoint(&base_url))
   352→        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))
   353→        .header(
   354→            "Authorization",
   355→            format!("Bearer {}", api_key.expose_secret()),
   356→        )
   357→        .header("Accept", "application/json")
   358→        .send()
   359→        .await?;
   360→    let status = response.status();
   361→    let body = response.text().await?;
   362→    if !status.is_success() {
   363→        anyhow::bail!("openai models API error HTTP {status}");
   364→    }
   365→    let payload: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)?;
   366→    let models = parse_models_payload(&payload);
   367→    if models.is_empty() {
   368→        anyhow::bail!("openai models API returned no models");
   369→    }
   370→    Ok(models)
   371→}
   372→
   373→/// Spawn model discovery in a background thread and return the receiver
   374→/// immediately, without blocking. Call `.recv()` later to collect the result.
   375→pub fn start_model_discovery(
   376→    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
   377→    base_url: String,
   378→) -> mpsc::Receiver<anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>>> {
   379→    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   380→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   381→        let result = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
   382→            .enable_all()
   383→            .build()
   384→            .map_err(anyhow::Error::from)
   385→            .and_then(|rt| rt.block_on(fetch_models_from_api(api_key, base_url)));
   386→        let _ = tx.send(result);
   387→    });
   388→    rx
   389→}
   390→
   391→fn fetch_models_blocking(
   392→    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
   393→    base_url: String,
   394→) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   395→    start_model_discovery(api_key, base_url)
   396→        .recv()
   397→        .map_err(|err| anyhow::anyhow!("openai model discovery worker failed: {err}"))?
   398→}
   399→
   400→pub fn live_models(
   401→    api_key: &secrecy::Secret<String>,
   402→    base_url: &str,
   403→) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   404→    let models = fetch_models_blocking(api_key.clone(), base_url.to_string())?;
   405→    debug!(model_count = models.len(), "loaded live models");
   406→    Ok(models)
   407→}
   408→
   409→#[must_use]
   410→pub fn available_models(
   411→    api_key: &secrecy::Secret<String>,
   412→    base_url: &str,
   413→) -> Vec<super::DiscoveredModel> {
   414→    let fallback = default_model_catalog();
   415→    if cfg!(test) {
   416→        return fallback;
   417→    }
   418→
   419→    let discovered = match live_models(api_key, base_url) {
   420→        Ok(models) => models,
   421→        Err(err) => {
   422→            warn!(error = %err, base_url = %base_url, "failed to fetch openai models, using fallback catalog");
   423→            return fallback;
   424→        },
   425→    };
   426→
   427→    let merged = super::merge_discovered_with_fallback_catalog(discovered, fallback);
   428→    debug!(model_count = merged.len(), "loaded openai models catalog");
   429→    merged
   430→}

   648→    fn split_responses_instructions_and_input(
   649→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   650→    ) -> (Option<String>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {
   651→        let mut instruction_parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   652→        let mut non_system: Vec<ChatMessage> = Vec::new();
   653→
   654→        for message in messages {
   655→            match message {
   656→                ChatMessage::System { content } => {
   657→                    if !content.trim().is_empty() {
   658→                        instruction_parts.push(content);
   659→                    }
   660→                },
   661→                other => non_system.push(other),
   662→            }
   663→        }
   664→
   665→        let instructions = if instruction_parts.is_empty() {
   666→            None
   667→        } else {
   668→            Some(instruction_parts.join("\n\n"))
   669→        };
   670→
   671→        (instructions, to_responses_input(&non_system))
   672→    }
   673→

   816→    fn stream_with_tools_websocket(
   817→        &self,
   818→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   819→        tools: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
   820→        fallback_to_sse: bool,
   821→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   822→        // Synchronous pre-flight: URL, request, auth header, pool key.
   823→        // Fail fast and fall back to SSE before entering the async generator,
   824→        // which avoids cloning messages/tools for the four sync-check paths.
   825→        let (request, pool_key) = match (|| -> crate::error::Result<_> {
   826→            if !self.is_openai_platform_base_url() {
   827→                return Err(crate::error::Error::message(format!(
   828→                    "websocket mode is only supported for api.openai.com (got {})",
   829→                    self.base_url
   830→                )));
   831→            }
   832→            let ws_url = self.responses_websocket_url()?;
   833→            let pk = super::ws_pool::PoolKey::new(&ws_url, &self.api_key);
   834→            let mut req = ws_url.as_str().into_client_request()?;
   835→            let auth = format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret());
   836→            req.headers_mut()
   837→                .insert("Authorization", HeaderValue::from_str(&auth)?);
   838→            req.headers_mut()
   839→                .insert("OpenAI-Beta", HeaderValue::from_static("responses=v1"));
   840→            Ok((req, pk))
   841→        })() {
   842→            Ok(r) => r,
   843→            Err(err) => {
   844→                if fallback_to_sse {
   845→                    debug!(error = %err, "websocket setup failed, falling back to sse");
   846→                    return self.stream_with_tools_sse(messages, tools);
   847→                }
   848→                return Box::pin(async_stream::stream! {
   849→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(err.to_string());
   850→                });
   851→            },
   852→        };
   853→
   854→        Box::pin(async_stream::stream! {
   855→            // Try the pool first; fall back to a fresh connection.
   856→            let (mut ws_stream, created_at) = if let Some(pooled) = super::ws_pool::shared_ws_pool().checkout(&pool_key).await {
   857→                pooled
   858→            } else {
   859→                match tokio_tungstenite::connect_async(request).await {
   860→                    Ok((ws, _)) => (ws, std::time::Instant::now()),
   861→                    Err(err) => {
   862→                        if fallback_to_sse {
   863→                            debug!(error = %err, "websocket connect failed, falling back to sse");
   864→                            let mut sse = self.stream_with_tools_sse(messages, tools);
   865→                            while let Some(event) = sse.next().await {
   866→                                yield event;
   867→                            }
   868→                        } else {
   869→                            yield StreamEvent::Error(err.to_string());
   870→                        }
   871→                        return;
   872→                    }
   873→                }
   874→            };
   875→
   876→            let (instructions, input) = Self::split_responses_instructions_and_input(messages);
   877→            let mut response_payload = serde_json::json!({
   878→                "model": self.model,
   879→                "stream": true,
   880→                "store": false,
   881→                "input": input,
   882→            });
   883→            if let Some(instructions) = instructions {
   884→                response_payload["instructions"] = serde_json::Value::String(instructions);
   885→            }
   886→            if !tools.is_empty() {
   887→                response_payload["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_responses_api_tools(&tools));
   888→                response_payload["tool_choice"] = serde_json::json!("auto");
   889→            }
   890→
   891→            self.apply_reasoning_effort_responses(&mut response_payload);
   892→
   893→            let create_event = serde_json::json!({
   894→                "type": "response.create",
   895→                "response": response_payload,
   896→            });
   897→
   898→            debug!(
   899→                model = %self.model,
   900→                tools_count = tools.len(),
   901→                reasoning_effort = ?self.reasoning_effort,
   902→                "openai stream_with_tools request (websocket)"
   903→            );
   904→            trace!(event = %create_event, "openai websocket create event");
   905→
   906→            if let Err(err) = ws_stream
   907→                .send(Message::Text(create_event.to_string().into()))
   908→                .await
   909→            {
   910→                yield StreamEvent::Error(format!("websocket send failed: {err}"));
   911→                return;
   912→            }
   913→
   914→            let mut input_tokens: u32 = 0;
   915→            let mut output_tokens: u32 = 0;
   916→            let mut current_tool_index: usize = 0;
   917→            let mut tool_calls: HashMap<usize, (String, String)> = HashMap::new();
   918→            let mut completed_tool_calls: HashSet<usize> = HashSet::new();
   919→            let mut clean_completion = false;
   920→
   921→            while let Some(frame) = ws_stream.next().await {
   922→                let text = match frame {
   923→                    Ok(Message::Text(t)) => t.to_string(),
   924→                    Ok(Message::Binary(b)) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&b).into_owned(),
   925→                    Ok(Message::Ping(p)) => {
   926→                        if let Err(err) = ws_stream.send(Message::Pong(p)).await {
   927→                            yield StreamEvent::Error(err.to_string());
   928→                            return;
   929→                        }
   930→                        continue;
   931→                    }
   932→                    Ok(Message::Close(_)) => break,
   933→                    Ok(_) => continue,
   934→                    Err(err) => {
   935→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(err.to_string());
   936→                        return;
   937→                    }
   938→                };
   939→
   940→                let Ok(evt) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else {
   941→                    continue;
   942→                };
   943→                trace!(event = %evt, "openai websocket event");
   944→
   945→                match evt["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("") {
   946→                    "response.output_text.delta" => {
   947→                        if let Some(delta) = evt["delta"].as_str()
   948→                            && !delta.is_empty()
   949→                        {
   950→                            yield StreamEvent::Delta(delta.to_string());
   951→                        }
   952→                    }
   953→                    "response.output_item.added" => {
   954→                        if evt["item"]["type"].as_str() == Some("function_call") {
   955→                            let id = evt["item"]["call_id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   956→                            let name = evt["item"]["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   957→                            let index = ws_output_index(&evt, current_tool_index);
   958→                            current_tool_index = current_tool_index.max(index + 1);
   959→                            tool_calls.insert(index, (id.clone(), name.clone()));
   960→                            yield StreamEvent::ToolCallStart { id, name, index };
   961→                        }
   962→                    }
   963→                    "response.function_call_arguments.delta" => {
   964→                        if let Some(delta) = evt["delta"].as_str()
   965→                            && !delta.is_empty()
   966→                        {
   967→                            let index = ws_output_index(&evt, current_tool_index.saturating_sub(1));
   968→                            yield StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta {
   969→                                index,
   970→                                delta: delta.to_string(),

  "new_string": "// ============================================================================\n// Responses API helpers (shared by openai.rs and github_copilot.rs)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// Split system messages into `instructions` and convert the rest to Responses\n/// API `input` items.\n///\n/// The Responses API uses a top-level `instructions` field instead of a system\n/// message role.  This function extracts all system messages, joins them with\n/// `\\n\\n`, and converts the remaining messages via [`to_responses_input`].\n#[must_use]\npub fn split_responses_instructions_and_input(\n    messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,\n) -> (Option<String>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {\n    let mut instruction_parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    let mut non_system: Vec<ChatMessage> = Vec::new();\n\n    for message in messages {\n        match message {\n            ChatMessage::System { content } => {\n                if !content.trim().is_empty() {\n                    instruction_parts.push(content);\n                }\n            },\n            other => non_system.push(other),\n        }\n    }\n\n    let instructions = if instruction_parts.is_empty() {\n        None\n    } else {\n        Some(instruction_parts.join(\"\\n\\n\"))\n    };\n\n    (instructions, to_responses_input(&non_system))\n}\n\n/// Resolve the output index from a Responses API event.\n///\n/// The Responses API uses `output_index` for items and `index` for\n/// sub-item fields.  WebSocket events may also use `item_index`.\n/// Falls back to `fallback` if none of these keys are present.\npub fn responses_output_index(event: &serde_json::Value, fallback: usize) -> usize {\n    event\n        .get(\"output_index\")\n        .or_else(|| event.get(\"item_index\"))\n        .or_else(|| event.get(\"index\"))\n        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_u64)\n        .map(|i| i as usize)\n        .unwrap_or(fallback)\n}\n\n/// State for tracking Responses API SSE streaming.\n#[derive(Default)]\npub struct ResponsesStreamState {\n    /// Map from index -> (call_id, name)\n    pub tool_calls: HashMap<usize, (String, String)>,\n    /// Set of tool call indices that have already emitted `ToolCallComplete`.\n    pub completed_tool_calls: HashSet<usize>,\n    /// The next tool call index to assign.\n    pub current_tool_index: usize,\n    pub input_tokens: u32,\n    pub output_tokens: u32,\n    pub cache_read_tokens: u32,\n    pub cache_write_tokens: u32,\n}\n\n/// Process a single SSE data line from a Responses API stream.\n///\n/// Returns [`SseLineResult`] indicating whether to skip, yield events, or stop.\n///\n/// Handles the event types emitted by the Responses API:\n/// - `response.output_text.delta` → text delta\n/// - `response.output_item.added` (type=function_call) → tool call start\n/// - `response.function_call_arguments.delta` → tool call arguments delta\n/// - `response.function_call_arguments.done` → tool call complete\n/// - `response.completed` → parse usage, done\n/// - `error` / `response.failed` → error\npub fn process_responses_sse_line(\n    data: &str,\n    state: &mut ResponsesStreamState,\n) -> SseLineResult {\n    if data == \"[DONE]\" {\n        return SseLineResult::Done;\n    }\n\n    let Ok(evt) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(data) else {\n        return SseLineResult::Skip;\n    };\n\n    match evt[\"type\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\") {\n        \"response.output_text.delta\" => {\n            if let Some(delta) = evt[\"delta\"].as_str()\n                && !delta.is_empty()\n            {\n                SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::Delta(delta.to_string())])\n            } else {\n                SseLineResult::Skip\n            }\n        },\n        \"response.output_item.added\" => {\n            if evt[\"item\"][\"type\"].as_str() == Some(\"function_call\") {\n                let id = evt[\"item\"][\"call_id\"]\n                    .as_str()\n                    .unwrap_or(\"\")\n                    .to_string();\n                let name = evt[\"item\"][\"name\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string();\n                let index = responses_output_index(&evt, state.current_tool_index);\n                state.current_tool_index = state.current_tool_index.max(index + 1);\n                state.tool_calls.insert(index, (id.clone(), name.clone()));\n                SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::ToolCallStart { id, name, index }])\n            } else {\n                SseLineResult::Skip\n            }\n        },\n        \"response.function_call_arguments.delta\" => {\n            if let Some(delta) = evt[\"delta\"].as_str()\n                && !delta.is_empty()\n            {\n                let index = responses_output_index(\n                    &evt,\n                    state.current_tool_index.saturating_sub(1),\n                );\n                SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta {\n                    index,\n                    delta: delta.to_string(),\n                }])\n            } else {\n                SseLineResult::Skip\n            }\n        },\n        \"response.function_call_arguments.done\" => {\n            let index =\n                responses_output_index(&evt, state.current_tool_index.saturating_sub(1));\n            if state.completed_tool_calls.insert(index) {\n                SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete { index }])\n            } else {\n                SseLineResult::Skip\n            }\n        },\n        \"response.completed\" => {\n            if let Some(usage) = evt\n                .get(\"response\")\n                .and_then(|response| response.get(\"usage\"))\n            {\n                let parsed = parse_openai_compat_usage(usage);\n                state.input_tokens = parsed.input_tokens;\n                state.output_tokens = parsed.output_tokens;\n                state.cache_read_tokens = parsed.cache_read_tokens;\n                state.cache_write_tokens = parsed.cache_write_tokens;\n            }\n            SseLineResult::Done\n        },\n        \"error\" | \"response.failed\" => {\n            let msg = evt[\"error\"][\"message\"]\n                .as_str()\n                .or_else(|| evt[\"response\"][\"error\"][\"message\"].as_str())\n                .or_else(|| evt[\"message\"].as_str())\n                .unwrap_or(\"unknown error\");\n            SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::Error(msg.to_string())])\n        },\n        _ => SseLineResult::Skip,\n    }\n}\n\n/// Generate the final events when a Responses API stream ends.\n///\n/// Emits `ToolCallComplete` for any pending tool calls and a final `Done` with\n/// accumulated usage.\npub fn finalize_responses_stream(state: &mut ResponsesStreamState) -> Vec<StreamEvent> {\n    let mut events = Vec::new();\n\n    let mut pending: Vec<usize> = state.tool_calls.keys().copied().collect();\n    pending.sort_unstable();\n    for index in pending {\n        if state.completed_tool_calls.insert(index) {\n            events.push(StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete { index });\n        }\n    }\n\n    events.push(StreamEvent::Done(Usage {\n        input_tokens: state.input_tokens,\n        output_tokens: state.output_tokens,\n        cache_read_tokens: state.cache_read_tokens,\n        cache_write_tokens: state.cache_write_tokens,\n    }));\n\n    events\n}\n\n/// Parse a non-streaming Responses API JSON response into [`CompletionResponse`].\n///\n/// The Responses API returns an `output` array containing `message` items\n/// (with `content[].text`) and `function_call` items (with `call_id`, `name`,\n/// `arguments`).\npub fn parse_responses_completion(resp: &serde_json::Value) -> CompletionResponse {\n    let mut text: Option<String> = None;\n    let mut tool_calls: Vec<ToolCall> = Vec::new();\n\n    if let Some(output) = resp.get(\"output\").and_then(|o| o.as_array()) {\n        for item in output {\n            match item[\"type\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\") {\n                \"message\" => {\n                    if let Some(content) = item.get(\"content\").and_then(|c| c.as_array()) {\n                        for part in content {\n                            if part[\"type\"].as_str() == Some(\"output_text\") {\n                                if let Some(t) = part[\"text\"].as_str() {\n                                    text = Some(\n                                        text.map_or_else(|| t.to_string(), |prev| prev + t),\n                                    );\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                },\n                \"function_call\" => {\n                    let id = item[\"call_id\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string();\n                    let name = item[\"name\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string();\n                    let args_str = item[\"arguments\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"{}\");\n                    let arguments =\n                        serde_json::from_str(args_str).unwrap_or(serde_json::json!({}));\n                    tool_calls.push(ToolCall {\n                        id,\n                        name,\n                        arguments,\n                    });\n                },\n                _ => {},\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let usage = resp\n        .get(\"usage\")\n        .map(parse_openai_compat_usage)\n        .unwrap_or_default();\n\n    CompletionResponse {\n        text,\n        tool_calls,\n        usage,\n    }\n}\n\n#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {"
}

}

   458→
   459→/// Returns `true` if an error body from the Chat Completions API indicates
   460→/// that the model only supports the Responses API.
   461→fn is_responses_api_required_error(body: &str) -> bool {

   465→
   466→// ── LlmProvider impl ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   467→
   468→#[async_trait]
   469→impl LlmProvider for GitHubCopilotProvider {
   470→    fn name(&self) -> &str {
   471→        PROVIDER_NAME
   472→    }
   473→
   474→    fn id(&self) -> &str {
   475→        &self.model
   476→    }
   477→
   478→    fn supports_tools(&self) -> bool {
   479→        super::supports_tools_for_model(&self.model)
   480→    }
   481→
   482→    async fn complete(
   483→        &self,
   484→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   485→        tools: &[serde_json::Value],
   486→    ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   487→        let token = self.get_valid_copilot_token().await?;
   488→
   489→        let openai_messages: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   490→            messages.iter().map(ChatMessage::to_openai_value).collect();
   491→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   492→            "model": self.model,
   493→            "messages": openai_messages,
   494→        });
   495→
   496→        if !tools.is_empty() {
   497→            body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(tools));
   498→        }
   499→
   500→        debug!(
   501→            model = %self.model,
   502→            messages_count = messages.len(),
   503→            tools_count = tools.len(),
   504→            "github-copilot complete request"
   505→        );
   506→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "github-copilot request body");
   507→
   508→        let http_resp = self
   509→            .client
   510→            .post(format!("{COPILOT_API_BASE}/chat/completions"))
   511→            .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {token}"))
   512→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   513→            .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   514→            .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   515→            .json(&body)
   516→            .send()
   517→            .await?;
   518→
   519→        let status = http_resp.status();
   520→        if !status.is_success() {
   521→            let retry_after_ms = super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   522→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   523→            warn!(status = %status, body = %body_text, "github-copilot API error");
   524→            anyhow::bail!(
   525→                "{}",
   526→                super::with_retry_after_marker(
   527→                    format!("GitHub Copilot API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   528→                    retry_after_ms,
   529→                )
   530→            );
   531→        }
   532→
   533→        let resp = http_resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;
   534→        trace!(response = %resp, "github-copilot raw response");
   535→
   536→        let message = &resp["choices"][0]["message"];
   537→
   538→        let text = message["content"].as_str().map(|s| s.to_string());
   539→        let tool_calls = parse_tool_calls(message);
   540→
   541→        let usage = parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(&resp).unwrap_or_default();
   542→
   543→        Ok(CompletionResponse {
   544→            text,
   545→            tool_calls,
   546→            usage,
   547→        })
   548→    }
   549→
   550→    #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   551→    fn stream(
   552→        &self,
   553→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   554→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   555→        self.stream_with_tools(messages, vec![])
   556→    }
   557→
   558→    #[allow(clippy::collapsible_if)]
   559→    fn stream_with_tools(
   560→        &self,
   561→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
   562→        tools: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
   563→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {
   564→        Box::pin(async_stream::stream! {
   565→            let token = match self.get_valid_copilot_token().await {
   566→                Ok(t) => t,
   567→                Err(e) => {
   568→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   569→                    return;
   570→                }
   571→            };
   572→
   573→            let openai_messages: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   574→                messages.iter().map(ChatMessage::to_openai_value).collect();
   575→            let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   576→                "model": self.model,
   577→                "messages": openai_messages,
   578→                "stream": true,
   579→                "stream_options": { "include_usage": true },
   580→            });
   581→
   582→            if !tools.is_empty() {
   583→                body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(&tools));
   584→            }
   585→
   586→            debug!(
   587→                model = %self.model,
   588→                messages_count = openai_messages.len(),
   589→                tools_count = tools.len(),
   590→                "github-copilot stream_with_tools request"
   591→            );
   592→            trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "github-copilot stream request body");
   593→
   594→            let resp = match self
   595→                .client
   596→                .post(format!("{COPILOT_API_BASE}/chat/completions"))
   597→                .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {token}"))
   598→                .header("content-type", "application/json")
   599→                .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)
   600→                .header("User-Agent", COPILOT_USER_AGENT)
   601→                .json(&body)
   602→                .send()
   603→                .await
   604→            {
   605→                Ok(r) => {
   606→                    if let Err(e) = r.error_for_status_ref() {
   607→                        let status = e.status().map(|s| s.as_u16()).unwrap_or(0);
   608→                        let retry_after_ms = super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(r.headers());
   609→                        let body_text = r.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   610→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(super::with_retry_after_marker(
   611→                            format!("HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   612→                            retry_after_ms,
   613→                        ));
   614→                        return;
   615→                    }
   616→                    r
   617→                }
   618→                Err(e) => {
   619→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   620→                    return;
   621→                }
   622→            };
   623→
   624→            let mut byte_stream = resp.bytes_stream();
   625→            let mut buf = String::new();
   626→            let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
   627→
   628→            while let Some(chunk) = byte_stream.next().await {
   629→                let chunk = match chunk {
   630→                    Ok(c) => c,
   631→                    Err(e) => {
   632→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   633→                        return;
   634→                    }
   635→                };
   636→                buf.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&chunk));
   637→
   638→                while let Some(pos) = buf.find('\n') {
   639→                    let line = buf[..pos].trim().to_string();
   640→                    buf = buf[pos + 1..].to_string();
   641→
   642→                    if line.is_empty() {
   643→                        continue;
   644→                    }
   645→
   646→                    let Some(data) = line
   647→                        .strip_prefix("data: ")
   648→                        .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   649→                    else {
   650→                        continue;
   651→                    };
   652→
   653→                    match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   654→                        SseLineResult::Done => {
   655→                            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   656→                                yield event;
   657→                            }
   658→                            return;
   659→                        }
   660→                        SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   661→                            for event in events {
   662→                                yield event;
   663→                            }
   664→                        }
   665→                        SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   666→                    }
   667→                }
   668→            }
   669→
   670→            let line = buf.trim().to_string();
   671→            if !line.is_empty()
   672→                && let Some(data) = line
   673→                    .strip_prefix("data: ")
   674→                    .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   675→            {
   676→                match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   677→                    SseLineResult::Done => {
   678→                        for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   679→                            yield event;
   680→                        }
   681→                        return;
   682→                    }
   683→                    SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   684→                        for event in events {
   685→                            yield event;
   686→                        }
   687→                    }
   688→                    SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   689→                }
   690→            }
   691→
   692→            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   693→                yield event;
   694→            }
   695→        })
   696→    }
   697→}
   698→
   699→#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]
   700→#[cfg(test)]
   701→mod tests {
   702→    use super::*;
   703→
   704→    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
   705→

          matches!(&events[0], StreamEvent::Error(msg) if msg == \"model overloaded\")\n                );\n            },\n            _ => panic!(\"Expected Events\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_responses_sse_unknown_type_skipped() {\n        let mut state = ResponsesStreamState::default();\n        let data = r#\"{\"type\":\"response.created\"}\"#;\n        assert!(matches!(\n            process_responses_sse_line(data, &mut state),\n            SseLineResult::Skip\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // ============================================================\n    // Tests for finalize_responses_stream\n    // ============================================================\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_finalize_responses_stream_with_pending_tools() {\n        let mut state = ResponsesStreamState::default();\n        state.tool_calls.insert(0, (\"call_1\".into(), \"test\".into()));\n        state.tool_calls.insert(1, (\"call_2\".into(), \"test2\".into()));\n        state.completed_tool_calls.insert(0); // only 0 is completed\n        state.input_tokens = 30;\n        state.output_tokens = 10;\n\n        let events = finalize_responses_stream(&mut state);\n        // Should emit ToolCallComplete for index 1 (pending) + Done\n        assert_eq!(events.len(), 2);\n        assert!(matches!(&events[0], StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete { index: 1 }));\n        assert!(matches!(\n            &events[1],\n            StreamEvent::Done(usage) if usage.input_tokens == 30 && usage.output_tokens == 10\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_finalize_responses_stream_no_tools() {\n        let mut state = ResponsesStreamState::default();\n        state.input_tokens = 10;\n        state.output_tokens = 5;\n\n        let events = finalize_responses_stream(&mut state);\n        assert_eq!(events.len(), 1);\n        assert!(matches!(\n            &events[0],\n            StreamEvent::Done(usage) if usage.input_tokens == 10 && usage.output_tokens == 5\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // ============================================================\n    // Tests for parse_responses_completion\n    // ============================================================\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_responses_completion_text() {\n        let resp = serde_json::json!({\n            \"output\": [{\n                \"type\": \"message\",\n                \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"output_text\", \"text\": \"Hello!\"}]\n            }],\n            \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 15, \"output_tokens\": 3}\n        });\n        let result = parse_responses_completion(&resp);\n        assert_eq!(result.text.as_deref(), Some(\"Hello!\"));\n        assert!(result.tool_calls.is_empty());\n        assert_eq!(result.usage.input_tokens, 15);\n        assert_eq!(result.usage.output_tokens, 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_responses_completion_tool_call() {\n        let resp = serde_json::json!({\n            \"output\": [{\n                \"type\": \"function_call\",\n                \"call_id\": \"call_abc\",\n                \"name\": \"read_file\",\n                \"arguments\": \"{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/tmp/test.txt\\\"}\"\n            }],\n            \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 20, \"output_tokens\": 10}\n        });\n        let result = parse_responses_completion(&resp);\n        assert!(result.text.is_none());\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls[0].id, \"call_abc\");\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls[0].name, \"read_file\");\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls[0].arguments[\"path\"], \"/tmp/test.txt\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_responses_completion_mixed() {\n        let resp = serde_json::json!({\n            \"output\": [\n                {\n                    \"type\": \"message\",\n                    \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                    \"content\": [{\"type\": \"output_text\", \"text\": \"Let me read that file.\"}]\n                },\n                {\n                    \"type\": \"function_call\",\n                    \"call_id\": \"call_1\",\n                    \"name\": \"read_file\",\n                    \"arguments\": \"{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/tmp/a.txt\\\"}\"\n                }\n            ],\n            \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 25, \"output_tokens\": 15}\n        });\n        let result = parse_responses_completion(&resp);\n        assert_eq!(result.text.as_deref(), Some(\"Let me read that file.\"));\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(result.tool_calls[0].name, \"read_file\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_responses_completion_no_output() {\n        let resp = serde_json::json!({\n            \"usage\": {\"input_tokens\": 5, \"output_tokens\": 0}\n        });\n        let result = parse_responses_completion(&resp);\n        assert!(result.text.is_none());\n        assert!(result.tool_calls.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_parse_responses_completion_no_usage() {\n        let resp = serde_json::json!({\n            \"output\": [{\n                \"type\": \"message\",\n                \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                \"content\": [{\"type\": \"output_text\", \"text\": \"Hi\"}]\n            }]\n        });\n        let result = parse_responses_completion(&resp);\n        assert_eq!(result.text.as_deref(), Some(\"Hi\"));\n        assert_eq!(result.usage.input_tokens, 0);\n        assert_eq!(result.usage.output_tokens, 0);\n    }\n}"

   835→                .header("Editor-Version", EDITOR_VERSION)

   837→                .json(&body)
   838→                .send()
   839→                .await
   840→            {
   841→                Ok(r) => {
   842→                    if let Err(e) = r.error_for_status_ref() {
   843→                        let status = e.status().map(|s| s.as_u16()).unwrap_or(0);
   844→                        let retry_after_ms = super::retry_after_ms_from_headers(r.headers());
   845→                        let body_text = r.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   846→
   847→                        // Fallback: if this is an unsupported API error,
   848→                        // switch to Responses API streaming.
   849→                        if status == 400
   850→                            && is_responses_api_required_error(&body_text)
   851→                        {
   852→                            debug!(

   854→                                "chat completions returned unsupported_api_for_model, \
   855→                                 falling back to responses API streaming"

   859→                            while let Some(event) = responses_stream.next().await {
   860→                                yield event;
   861→                            }
   862→                            return;
   863→                        }
   864→

   866→                            format!("HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   867→                            retry_after_ms,

   869→                        return;

   879→            let mut byte_stream = resp.bytes_stream();
   880→            let mut buf = String::new();
   881→            let mut state = StreamingToolState::default();
   882→
   883→            while let Some(chunk) = byte_stream.next().await {
   884→                let chunk = match chunk {
   885→                    Ok(c) => c,
   886→                    Err(e) => {
   887→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
   888→                        return;
   889→                    }
   890→                };
   891→                buf.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&chunk));
   892→
   893→                while let Some(pos) = buf.find('\n') {
   894→                    let line = buf[..pos].trim().to_string();
   895→                    buf = buf[pos + 1..].to_string();
   896→
   897→                    if line.is_empty() {
   898→                        continue;
   899→                    }
   900→
   901→                    let Some(data) = line
   902→                        .strip_prefix("data: ")
   903→                        .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   904→                    else {
   905→                        continue;
   906→                    };
   907→
   908→                    match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   909→                        SseLineResult::Done => {
   910→                            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   911→                                yield event;
   912→                            }
   913→                            return;
   914→                        }
   915→                        SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   916→                            for event in events {
   917→                                yield event;
   918→                            }
   919→                        }
   920→                        SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   921→                    }
   922→                }
   923→            }
   924→
   925→            let line = buf.trim().to_string();
   926→            if !line.is_empty()
   927→                && let Some(data) = line
   928→                    .strip_prefix("data: ")
   929→                    .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   930→            {
   931→                match process_openai_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   932→                    SseLineResult::Done => {
   933→                        for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   934→                            yield event;
   935→                        }
   936→                        return;
   937→                    }
   938→                    SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   939→                        for event in events {
   940→                            yield event;
   941→                        }
   942→                    }
   943→                    SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   944→                }
   945→            }
   946→
   947→            for event in finalize_stream(&mut state) {
   948→                yield event;
   949→            }
   950→        })
   951→    }

   963→    /// Captured request data for assertions.
   964→    #[derive(Default, Clone)]
   965→    struct CapturedRequest {
   966→        headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
   967→        body: Option<serde_json::Value>,
   968→    }
   969→
   970→    /// Start a mock HTTP server, returning (base_url, captured_requests).
   971→    async fn start_mock_with_capture(
   972→        response_body: serde_json::Value,
   973→    ) -> (String, Arc<Mutex<Vec<CapturedRequest>>>) {
   974→        let captured: Arc<Mutex<Vec<CapturedRequest>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
   975→        let captured_clone = captured.clone();
   976→        let resp_body = response_body.clone();
   977→
   978→        let app = Router::new().route(
   979→            "/chat/completions",
   980→            post(move |req: Request| {
   981→                let cap = captured_clone.clone();
   982→                let resp = resp_body.clone();
   983→                async move {
   984→                    let headers: Vec<(String, String)> = req
   985→                        .headers()
   986→                        .iter()
   987→                        .map(|(k, v)| {
   988→                            (k.as_str().to_string(), v.to_str().unwrap_or("").to_string())
   989→                        })
   990→                        .collect();
   991→
   992→                    let body_bytes = axum::body::to_bytes(req.into_body(), 1024 * 1024)
   993→                        .await
   994→                        .unwrap_or_default();
   995→                    let body: Option<serde_json::Value> = serde_json::from_slice(&body_bytes).ok();
   996→
   997→                    cap.lock().unwrap().push(CapturedRequest { headers, body });
   998→
   999→                    axum::Json(resp)
  1000→                }
  1001→            }),
  1002→        );
  1003→
  1004→        let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
  1005→        let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
  1006→        tokio::spawn(async move {
  1007→            axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
  1008→        });
  1009→
  1010→        (format!("http://{addr}"), captured)
  1011→    }

> AGENT

All tasks complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

### `crates/providers/src/openai_compat.rs` — new shared utilities
- **`split_responses_instructions_and_input()`** — extracted from `OpenAiProvider` as a free function. Splits system messages into `instructions` and converts the rest via `to_responses_input()`.
- **`responses_output_index()`** — resolves `output_index`/`item_index`/`index` from Responses API events (extracted from `ws_output_index()`).
- **`ResponsesStreamState`** — state struct for tracking Responses API SSE streaming (tool calls, completed set, usage tokens).
- **`process_responses_sse_line()`** — processes a single SSE `data:` line from a Responses API stream. Handles all event types: text deltas, tool call lifecycle, completion with usage, errors.
- **`finalize_responses_stream()`** — emits pending `ToolCallComplete` events + `Done` with usage.
- **`parse_responses_completion()`** — parses a non-streaming Responses API JSON response into `CompletionResponse`.

### `crates/providers/src/openai.rs` — uses shared functions
- Replaced `Self::split_responses_instructions_and_input()` → `split_responses_instructions_and_input()` (free function)
- Replaced `ws_output_index()` → `responses_output_index()` (shared function)
- Removed the local implementations

### `crates/providers/src/github_copilot.rs` — Responses API support
- **`needs_responses_api()`** — returns `true` for `gpt-5.4*`, `gpt-5.2-pro`, `codex-*`
- **`is_responses_api_required_error()`** — detects `unsupported_api_for_model` errors
- **`complete()`** — routes to `complete_responses()` for known models; falls back on 400 error
- **`complete_responses()`** — non-streaming via `/responses`
- **`stream_with_tools()`** — routes to `stream_responses_api()` for known models
- **`stream_chat_completions()`** — existing chat completions streaming with error-based fallback
- **`stream_responses_api()`** — streaming via `/responses` with SSE
- **`COPILOT_MODELS`** — added `gpt-5.4`, `gpt-5.4-pro`, `gpt-5.2-pro`

### Tests — 28 new tests (186 → 214)
- `openai_compat.rs`: tests for `split_responses_instructions_and_input`, `responses_output_index`, `process_responses_sse_line` (all event types), `finalize_responses_stream`, `parse_responses_completion`
- `github_copilot.rs`: tests for `needs_responses_api`, `is_responses_api_required_error`, `complete_responses` with mock server, tools in Responses API format, system message → instructions

This function extracts all system messages, joins them with\n+/// `\\n\\n`, and converts the remaining messages via [`to_responses_input`].\n+#[must_use]\n+pub fn split_responses_instructions_and_input(\n+    messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,\n+) -> (Option<String>, Vec<serde_json::Value>) {\n+    let mut instruction_parts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n+    let mut non_system: Vec<ChatMessage> = Vec::new();\n+\n+    for message in messages {\n+        match message {\n+            ChatMessage::System { content } => {\n+                if !content.trim().is_empty() {\n+                    instruction_parts.push(content);\n+                }\n+            },\n+            other => non_system.push(other),\n+        }\n+    }\n+\n+    let instructions = if instruction_parts.is_empty() {\n+        None\n+    } else {\n+        Some(instruction_parts.join(\"\\n\\n\"))\n+    };\n+\n+    (instructions, to_responses_input(&non_system))\n+}\n+\n+/// Resolve the output index from a Responses API event.\n+///\n+/// The Responses API uses `output_index` for items and `index` for\n+/// sub-item fields. evt[\"type\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\") {\n+        \"response.output_text.delta\" => {\n+            if let Some(delta) = evt[\"delta\"].as_str()\n+                && !delta.is_empty()\n+            {\n+                SseLineResult::Events(vec![StreamEvent::Delta(delta.to_string())])\n+            } else {\n+                SseLineResult::Skip\n+            }\n+        },\n+        \"response.output_item.added\" => {\n+            if evt[\"item\"][\"type\"].as_str() == Some(\"function_call\") {\n+                let id = Any consumer that subscribes to `ProviderRaw` for raw-passthrough, debug tooling, or proxy use-cases will receive nothing when the Copilot provider routes through the Responses API SSE path, while the Chat Completions path for the same provider does surface those events.\n\nThe OpenAI WebSocket path in `openai.rs` also omits `ProviderRaw`, so there is arguably prior art for this omission in Summary</h3>\n\nThis PR adds Responses API (`/responses`) support to the GitHub Copilot provider for newer models (`gpt-5.4`, `gpt-5.4-pro`, `gpt-5.2-pro`, `codex-*`) that do not support `/chat/completions`, and extracts the shared Responses API helpers into `openai_compat.rs` for reuse across both the Copilot and OpenAI providers.\n\nKey changes:\n- `github_copilot.rs`: `needs_responses_api` performs model-based routing; `is_responses_api_required_error` provides error-based fallback for unknown future models; new `complete_responses` and `stream_responses_api` methods implement non-streaming and streaming paths respectively.\n- `openai_compat.rs`: Adds `ResponsesStreamState`, `process_responses_sse_line`, `finalize_responses_stream`, and `parse_responses_completion` as shared, tested utilities. `ResponsesStreamState` correctly tracks all four usage token types including cache read/write, which is an improvement over the existing inline WebSocket handler.\n- `openai.rs`: Imports `to_responses_api_tools` (now shared) for use in the existing WebSocket Responses API handler.\n\nThree minor issues were found:\n- `is_responses_api_required_error` in `github_copilot.rs` uses case-sensitive matching, unlike the analogous helper in `openai.rs` — if the Copilot API ever varies the casing of its error message the fallback would silently fail.\n- `process_responses_sse_line` does not emit `ProviderRaw` events, which `process_openai_sse_line` does; this may affect consumers that depend on raw event passthrough.\n- The existing `stream_with_tools_websocket` in `openai.rs` was not refactored to use the new shared `ResponsesStreamState`, leaving cache token fields zeroed in that path while the new Copilot SSE path reports them correctly.\n\n<h3>Confidence Score: 4/5</h3>\n\n- This PR is safe to merge with minor style improvements recommended.\n- The core routing logic, fallback mechanism, and shared utilities are all well-structured and covered by 28 new tests. The deducted point reflects three non-critical inconsistencies: case-sensitive error matching in the fallback check, missing `ProviderRaw` events in the SSE Responses path, and the pre-existing WebSocket handler not being updated to use the new shared `ResponsesStreamState` (leaving cache tokens zeroed there). None of these are correctness regressions for the newly added functionality.\n- Pay close attention to `crates/providers/src/github_copilot.rs` (`is_responses_api_required_error`) and the `stream_with_tools_websocket` handler in `crates/providers/src/openai.rs`.\n\n<h3>Important Files Changed</h3>\n\n\n\n\n| Filename | Overview |\n|----------|----------|\n| crates/providers/src/github_copilot.rs | Adds Responses API support via `needs_responses_api` routing and error-based fallback; logic is sound overall, though the fallback error detection uses case-sensitive matching inconsistent with similar helpers elsewhere in the codebase. |\n| crates/providers/src/openai_compat.rs | Adds well-structured shared Responses API helpers (`ResponsesStreamState`, `process_responses_sse_line`, `finalize_responses_stream`, `parse_responses_completion`). Cache token tracking is correct in `ResponsesStreamState`, but `process_responses_sse_line` does not emit `ProviderRaw` events, unlike `process_openai_sse_line`. |\n| crates/providers/src/openai.rs | Only change is adding `to_responses_api_tools` to the import list and using it in `stream_with_tools_websocket`; functionally correct, but the existing inline WebSocket event-processing loop was not refactored to use the new shared `ResponsesStreamState`, leaving cache-token tracking absent from that path. |\n\n</details>\n\n\n\n<h3>Flowchart</h3>\n\n```mermaid\n%%{init: {'theme': 'neutral'}}%%\nflowchart TD\n    A[Request: complete / stream_with_tools] --> B{needs_responses_api\\nmodel?}\n    B -- Yes --> C[Responses API path\\n/responses]\n    B -- No --> D[Chat Completions path\\n/chat/completions]\n\n    D --> E{HTTP 400 +\\nunsupported_api_for_model?}\n    E -- Yes --> F[Fallback to\\nResponses API path]\n    E -- No --> G[Return error to caller]\n\n    C --> H{Streaming?}\n    H -- Yes --> I[stream_responses_api\\nSSE stream]\n    H -- No --> J[complete_responses\\nJSON response]\n\n    F --> H\n\n    I --> K[process_responses_sse_line\\nopenai_compat.rs]\n    K --> L{Event type}\n    L -- response.output_text.delta --> M[StreamEvent::Delta]\n    L -- response.output_item.added\\nfunction_call --> N[StreamEvent::ToolCallStart]\n    L -- response.function_call_arguments.delta --> O[StreamEvent::ToolCallArgumentsDelta]\n    L -- response.function_call_arguments.done --> P[StreamEvent::ToolCallComplete]\n    L -- response.completed --> Q[Parse usage + Done]\n    L -- error / response.failed --> R[StreamEvent::Error]\n\n    J --> S[parse_responses_completion\\nopenai_compat.rs]\n    S --> T[CompletionResponse]\n```\n\n<!-- greptile_failed_comments -->\n<details open><summary><h3>Comments Outside Diff (1)</h3></summary>\n\n1. `crates/providers/src/openai.rs`, line 945-957 ([link](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/blob/99b2c41405401f1804e8f8b788063fa6f98d0cae/crates/providers/src/openai.rs#L945-L957)) \n\n   **Cache tokens silently dropped in WebSocket Responses API path**\n\n   This PR introduces `ResponsesStreamState` in `openai_compat.rs`, which correctly extracts and propagates `cache_read_tokens` and `cache_write_tokens` from the `response.completed` usage object. However, the existing inline `response.completed` handler here only captures `input_tokens` and `output_tokens`:\n\n   ```rust\n   let parsed = parse_openai_compat_usage(usage);\n   input_tokens = parsed.input_tokens;\n   output_tokens = parsed.output_tokens;\n   // cache_read_tokens / cache_write_tokens are never set\n   ```\n\n   The final `StreamEvent::Done` consequently always emits zeros for cache token fields (`..Default::default()`). This creates an inconsistency where the new Copilot SSE Responses path reports cache tokens correctly but the OpenAI WebSocket path does not. Since the new shared utilities were specifically designed to fix this kind of gap, consider refactoring this handler to use `ResponsesStreamState` for consistency.\n\n</details>\n\n<!-- /greptile_failed_comments -->\n\n<sub>Last reviewed commit: 99b2c41</sub>","author_association":"CONTRIBUTOR","pin":null,"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/issues/comments/4036876055/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"performed_via_github_app":{"id":867647,"client_id":"Iv1.5ad00a8cea2f6701","slug":"greptile-apps","node_id":"A_kwHOCFqEf84ADT0_","owner":{"login":"greptileai","id":140149887,"node_id":"O_kgDOCFqEfw","avatar_url":"https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/140149887?v=4","gravatar_id":"","url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai","html_url":"https://github.com/greptileai","followers_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/followers","following_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/following{/other_user}","gists_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/gists{/gist_id}","starred_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/starred{/owner}{/repo}","subscriptions_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/subscriptions","organizations_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/orgs","repos_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/repos","events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/events{/privacy}","received_events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/greptileai/received_events","type":"Organization","user_view_type":"public","site_admin":false},"name":"Greptile Apps","description":"Greptile is an AI agent that reviews pull requests with full context of your codebase. Done. Fixed the one actionable comment:

1. **Case-insensitive error matching** — `is_responses_api_required_error` now uses `to_ascii_lowercase()` before checking substrings, matching the pattern in `openai.rs`. Added a test for mixed-casing.

The other comment (missing `ProviderRaw` events) is a deliberate design choice — the OpenAI WebSocket Responses API path has the same behavior, so this is consistent.
